#### LH-13 — The minimap row's CLI path reads in its own shown sense: minimap.hide -> minimap.shown (launcher-§3, v2.65.0) ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-LH, LH-12, WS-06, LH-00
- **Findings** —
- **Change** The stored key stays `db.global.minimap.hide`, so no SavedVariables change and no migration. settings/Schema.lua: `minimapPath = "minimap.shown"` (:91). The stamp key `["minimap.hide"]` becomes `["minimap.shown"]` (:213), and its get and set still invert onto mm.hide and call NS.Launcher:SetShown. RESET_EXEMPT (:434) becomes a map from row path to stored path: `S.RESET_EXEMPT = { ["minimap.shown"] = "minimap.hide" }`. Keys are what the library's resetExempt veto and traceSettingsReset test; values are what the raw wipe carries. S:Register (:618-625): `defaultsRoot = function(_, row) if row and type(row.get) == "function" and type(row.set) == "function" then return nil end return g, 1 end`, so a row that owns its storage is neither resolved nor missing (the library's resolvesInDefaults answers nil). The host stub's R.Validate (:565-578) gets the same skip. settings/Slash.lua wipeGlobal (:119-131): `for _, storedPath in pairs(S.RESET_EXEMPT) do kept[storedPath] = S:ReadPath(g, storedPath) end`, with the write-back loop unchanged. Rewrite the comments at Schema.lua:79-91, :205-213 and :417-434, Slash.lua:113-118, :148 and :377-378, and OptionsSetup.lua:182: the row path is minimap.shown, the one stored key is minimap.hide, and no `shown` key is ever stored (anti-pattern #81). Tests: every `"minimap.hide"` used as a schema path in tests/test_launcher.lua, test_panel.lua, test_schema.lua, test_slash.lua and test_util.lua moves to "minimap.shown" with the value sense inverted where Set or Get is asserted. Assertions on the raw NS.db.global.minimap.hide stay. Docs: settings-panel.md:193, schema.md, slash-dispatch.md, smoke-tests.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, module-map.md and message-bus.md name the CLI path minimap.shown and the stored key minimap.hide. The commit body carries the release note: `/lh set minimap.hide` macros must become `/lh set minimap.shown <inverted>`.  Minimap rename carry-over (WS-06): the stored LibDBIcon key db.global.minimap.hide does NOT move, so every existing player keeps their setting with no SavedVariables migration and no schema-version bump (a stored `shown` key would violate anti-pattern #81). Pin it with a test written first: a legacy store with minimap = { hide = true, minimapPos = 200 } reads `<slash> get <path>.minimap.shown` as false, the button stays hidden, minimapPos is untouched, and no `shown` key is ever written to the raw SV after a set. Record the player-facing CLI rename (`...minimap.hide` -> `...minimap.shown`, old path answers unknown setting) in the commit body for the next Version History roll.

#### LH-15 — Schema seam: declare writeThrough {settings.enabled}; the host stub gains writeThrough storage and the full SetMany (Schema minor 2 parity)

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-LH, LH-13, LK-22, LH-00
- **Findings** —
- **Change** settings/Schema.lua. The SchemaLib:New descriptor (:587-595) gains `writeThrough = { "settings.enabled" }` (LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 minor 2, LK-23; WS-02 route (a)), a data list only, with no row, label, default or validate. hostSchemaStub (:452-583), stubLib:New(d): build `local wt = {}; for _, p in ipairs(d.writeThrough or {}) do wt[p] = true end` once. R.Set: when no row is found and `wt[path]`, resolve the root. It refuses with 'nowhere to store' when that is not a table; otherwise it does stubLib.Write(root, path, copy(value), first) and returns true, with no validate and no onChange, as the library does. Every other row-less path still refuses 'unknown path'. Add `R.SetMany(entries, opts)`, the library's all-or-nothing batch. Phase 1: for each entry, find the row (or the writeThrough path) and run validate, and return `false, err, nil, i` on the first failure before any store. Phase 2: R.Set each in order and return true. When opts.act is given it runs inside R.BulkBegin/BulkEnd so the sweep veto sees the bracket. If RV-LH already added a minimal SetMany to keep the parity case green, this replaces it. Also align R.Get and R.ApplyDefault with minor 2's instanceId forwarding: `R.Get(path, instanceId)` calls row.get(instanceId), and `R.ApplyDefault(row, instanceId)` forwards to Set. LootHistory rows ignore the id. Update the stub's header comment (:436-451): the writeThrough and SetMany semantics and the version-2 doc it mirrors. docs/schema.md: a writeThrough paragraph.  Upstream LK-22 (LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 minor 2): this item is the consumer follow-up that gives the host schema instance stub the SetMany member (with LK-22's all-or-nothing semantics and log-silence) plus the instanceId forwarding in R.Get/R.ApplyDefault, so the existing surface-parity case against the live v1.56.0 instance, which lists SetMany, stays green.

It loads the degraded env (tests/degraded_env.lua, as test_surface_parity does). (a) degradedNS.SchemaRuntime.Set('settings.enabled', false) returns true and stores false in the degraded db. Red today: the stub answers 'unknown path' because the hollow composer emitted no row. (b) Set('settings.nope', 1) still refuses. (c) SetMany over {valid, invalid} stores nothing and returns false with index 2; over two valid entries it stores both. (d) Live instance: Set('settings.enabled', ...) still takes the composed row (its onChange runs; spy NS.OnEnabledChanged). The existing parity case 'Schema stub instance carries every member of the live runtime' must stay green.

#### LH-19 — Measure, then memoize the AH priority-tag parse and bucket accumulateTime's date() calls

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-LH, LH-00
- **Findings** LootHistory-R-06
- **Change** Step 1, uncommitted: a scratch script (in the session scratchpad, not the repo) loads the harness, builds 50,000 synthetic records with auctionPrice maps, and times Database:Stats and Export:CSV before and after. Record the numbers in the commit body. If Pick plus accumulateTime is under 5% of Stats time, land only the step-3 test and a one-line comment at AuctionPrice:Pick recording the measurement, and close the finding as measured-no-action. Step 2, otherwise: modules/AuctionPrice.lua (:88-100) adds module-local `TAG_PROV, TAG_KEY = {}, {}` and `local function splitTag(tag)`, which caches tag:match('^(.-):(.+)$') per tag string. Tags are immutable strings, so there is no invalidation, no bus target and no stand-down change. Pick calls splitTag. core/Database.lua accumulateTime (:534-544) takes a per-Stats-call memo, created in newAccumulator as `A.timeMemo = {}`, keyed by `math.floor(r.ts / 900)`. Real timezone offsets are multiples of 15 minutes, so a 900 s bucket never straddles a local day or hour. The memo value holds {day, hour, wday} from one date('*t') plus string.format('%04d-%02d-%02d'), so a repeated bucket makes no date() call. Drop the finding's events-frames-taint-§7 citation from any comment (Stats is user-initiated, not per-frame).
- **Test first** Test first (step 3, always). tests/test_auctionprice.lua: Pick honors a reorder. With map {tsm={dbmarket=5}, auctionator={price=9}}, Pick answers the first tag in the priority; after MovePriorityWithin puts the other first, Pick answers it. It passes today and pins the memo against staleness. If step 2 lands, tests/test_stats.lua adds a case: Stats over records at 10:00, 10:05 and a local-midnight boundary gives the same byDay, byHour and byWeekday as today's implementation, computed in the case via date().
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/LootHistory && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list > docs/test-cases.md (+1 or +2; README badge) && lizard -l lua -x './libs/*' -x './tests/_kit/*' -C 15 -w . #### LH-29 — Ratify the deconstruct name-matching with a localization-§4 register row

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-LH, LH-10, LH-00
- **Findings** LootHistory-A-17
- **Change** docs/ARCHITECTURE.md ## Documented deviations: add the row. Rule `localization-§4`. What differs: modules/Attribution.lua resolves deconstruct casts by comparing the cast's client-locale name against names derived from seed spellIDs (NAME_SEEDS :20-50, seedToken via NS.Compat.GetSpellName :64, match :94), rather than by spellID alone. Why: the per-herb and per-ore Mass Mill and Mass Prospect ids are not enumerable; the names are derived from ids on the same client, so there is no English literal and the match is locale-correct by construction. Cite closed issue #2. Decided on the commit date. Re-check trigger: Blizzard exposes a stable deconstruct token or category, or the Mass Mill/Prospect id set becomes enumerable. Add a comment at Attribution.lua:20 pointing at the row.

#### AT-06 — Migration stamp per WS-03: defaults declare schemaVersion 0, the runner stamps only past a step that returned, and the profile-scoped v2 step runs for every stored profile ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-AT, WS-03
- **Findings** —
- **Change** (1) defaults/Profile.lua :103-115: `NS.defaults.global.schemaVersion = 0`. Rewrite the comment with the savedvariables-§1 (v2.65.0) reasons: AceDB's removeDefaults strips a stored value equal to its default, and AceDB backfills a declared default onto a legacy account; 0 has neither problem. Leave the per-profile stamp (:68, default 1, register row savedvariables-§1) unchanged. (2) core/Database.lua: publish `NS.SCHEMA_VERSION = SCHEMA_STEPS[#SCHEMA_STEPS].to`, derived and never a second literal, plus the suite seam `NS.__migrationSteps = SCHEMA_STEPS`. RunMigrations: `g.schemaVersion = g.schemaVersion or 0`. For each step with `g.schemaVersion < step.to`, run `local ok, err = pcall(step.apply, profile)`. On failure, emit `NS.Debug('Migrate', 'v%s -> v%s failed: %s', ...)` and one chat line `NS.Print('Settings upgrade stopped at v' .. from .. ' -> v' .. step.to .. '; saved settings were left as they were')`, and `break`, so the stamp does not advance and later steps do not run. On success, log as today and stamp `step.to`. (3) The v2 step (:164-167) is profile-scoped and was gated by the account-wide stamp. Make it `forEachProfile(function(p) if type(p) ~= 'table' or p.updateInterval == nil then return false end; p.updateInterval = nil; return true end)` with a Debug count line, like v4/v5. (4) docs: ARCHITECTURE.md's '### The per-profile schema stamp, in full' and savedvariables register row text, docs/schema.md's migration section, and CLAUDE.md if it quotes 'schemaVersion = 1'.
- **Test first** Red-first in tests/test_database.lua. (a) `assertEqual(NS.defaults.global.schemaVersion, 0)` (red today: 1). (b) Build db with global {schemaVersion=1} and sv.profiles {Default={updateInterval=0.1}, Raid={updateInterval=0.2}}, then RunMigrations: neither profile keeps updateInterval (red today: Raid keeps it). (c) Append `{ to = NS.SCHEMA_VERSION + 1, apply = function() error('boom') end }` to NS.__migrationSteps with global.schemaVersion = NS.SCHEMA_VERSION. RunMigrations does not raise (pcall-wrapped assert), the stamp stays NS.SCHEMA_VERSION, and one chat line names the stop. Remove the step in cleanup (-- red under: stamping before apply, or no pcall). (d) An existing case: a nil stamp still reaches 5. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.

#### AT-07 — Every event registration goes through LibKa0s-Core's SafeRegister helpers; rejected names are kept and surfaced by /at debug events ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-AT, LK-11, LK-04
- **Findings** AbsorbTracker-A-12
- **Change** (1) core/CoreSetup.lua. Live arm: `NS.SafeRegisterEvent = lib.SafeRegisterEvent`, `NS.SafeRegisterUnitEvent = lib.SafeRegisterUnitEvent`, `NS.SafeRegisterEvents = lib.SafeRegisterEvents`. Degraded arm (inside `if not lib`): three one-rung bodies with no front gate, per docs/api/Core/version-8-docs.md 'Degradation'. A local appendOnce(rejected, name) skips duplicates. SafeRegisterEvent does `local ok = pcall(target.RegisterEvent, target, event, handler); if not ok then appendOnce(rejected, event) end; return ok`. SafeRegisterUnitEvent(frame, event, rejected, u1, u2) is the same over RegisterUnitEvent. SafeRegisterEvents walks the array and returns the count. test_surface_parity's Core case derives from `^NS.X =` lines, so it covers the three names automatically. (2) core/State.lua: `NS.State.rejectedEvents = NS.State.rejectedEvents or {}` (session). (3) core/AbsorbTracker.lua. RegisterLifecycleEvents (:119-123): three `NS.SafeRegisterEvent(self, '<EVENT>', '<Method>', NS.State.rejectedEvents)` calls, driven from a local {event, method} array. SyncUnitEventFrames: `NS.SafeRegisterUnitEvent(f, 'UNIT_ABSORB_AMOUNT_CHANGED', rejected, unit)` and the same for UNIT_MAXHEALTH (:164-165), and the two swap registrations (:176, :181) via NS.SafeRegisterEvent. Each false return emits `NS.Debug('Events', 'rejected %s', event)`. That is 7 call sites; StandUp inherits them through the two methods. Update the comment at :114-117. core/Lifecycle.lua ADDON_EVENTS comment (:63-67): name the helper. (4) settings/Slash.lua runDebug: add sub-verb `events`, which prints 'Rejected events: none' or the comma-joined list, through a small sub-verb table so runDebug stays at CCN 10 or below. Help row: '`on`/`off` logging, `events`'. (5) core/DebugLogSetup.lua initSummary: append ', rejected events: <n>' when the list is non-empty. (6) docs/midnight-quirks.md: a note that a retired or unknown event name raises at registration (events-frames-taint-§1), and that the helper front-gates with C_EventUtils.IsEventValid where present. Add docs/ARCHITECTURE.md '## Event Subscriptions' and docs/slash-dispatch.md rows for `/at debug events`.
- **Test first** Red-first in a new suite tests/test_events.lua, listed in tests/run.lua immediately before test_disabled. (a) With T.mocks.__badEvents = { PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD = true }, NS.addon:RegisterLifecycleEvents() does not raise; PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED and PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED are still registered (T.mocks.__registrations()); NS.State.rejectedEvents lists PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD once, and still once after a second call (-- red under: a bare self:RegisterEvent). (b) Frame half, kit 26 (frame RegisterUnitEvent honors __badEvents): with __badEvents = { UNIT_MAXHEALTH = true }, SyncUnitEventFrames leaves UNIT_ABSORB_AMOUNT_CHANGED registered on the player frame and rejects UNIT_MAXHEALTH (-- red under: bare f:RegisterUnitEvent). (c) With C_EventUtils.IsEventValid mocked false for one name, that name is rejected without a RegisterEvent call. (d) `/at debug events` prints the list, and 'none' after cleanup. Cleanup clears __badEvents and rejectedEvents and re-runs RegisterLifecycleEvents/SyncUnitEventFrames so later suites see the full set. In tests/test_coresetup.lua: the degraded arm (loadDegraded) SafeRegisterEvent on a target whose RegisterEvent errors returns false and appends once (-- red under: a stub body without pcall). test_disabled stays green. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.

Rewrite the comment: the CLI name reads SHOWN (launcher-§3 v2.65.0), storage stays LibDBIcon's `minimap.hide`, the row's get/set invert (core/Data.lua), and no `shown` key is ever stored (anti-pattern #81). Update the :228-231 comment. resetExempt and reachedByProfileReset key off the constant and need no change. (3) The core/Data.lua comments (:45-53) name the new path. The settings/OptionsSetup.lua :59 comment names the new path. defaults/Profile.lua minimap.hide = false is unchanged. (4) Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md :128, :322, :378-392; docs/settings-panel.md :228, :257; docs/slash-dispatch.md :130; README.md if it lists paths. No SavedVariables migration.  Minimap rename carry-over (WS-06): the stored LibDBIcon key db.global.minimap.hide does NOT move, so every existing player keeps their setting with no SavedVariables migration and no schema-version bump (a stored `shown` key would violate anti-pattern #81). Pin it with a test written first: a legacy store with minimap = { hide = true, minimapPos = 200 } reads `<slash> get <path>.minimap.shown` as false, the button stays hidden, minimapPos is untouched, and no `shown` key is ever written to the raw SV after a set. #### AT-15 — Evaluate O.RenderTabbedSchema's v1.56.0 opts for the Appearance page's hand-built strip: adopt, or file a state:triaged decline ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-AT, LK-28, WS-06
- **Findings** AbsorbTracker-A-04
- **Change** settings/UnitPanel.lua renderUnitPanelBody step 2 (:313-343) hand-builds partitionTabs, then Helpers.TabStrip, then RenderRows or renderMirroredHint. Read LibKa0s docs/api/Options/version-24.31.4.7.4-docs.md (RenderTabbedSchema opts). ADOPT only if all four hold: (i) a PageHeader drawn BEFORE the call survives, and its ctx.__bannerHeight still feeds the strip's reservation; (ii) rows come from descriptor rowsForPage(pageKey, ctx.unit); (iii) disabledFor(cfg) is re-evaluated on every render and tab click; (iv) skipRender rows stay out of the strip. If adopting: replace step 2 with `Helpers.RenderTabbedSchema(ctx, pageKey, nil, <per the doc>, { disabledFor = function() return NS.Units.IsMirrored(ctx.unit) end, disabledNotice = 'Linked to Player <em dash> uncheck to customize.' })`, and delete partitionTabs, Helpers.__partitionTabs, renderMirroredHint and the onSelect closure. Keep the step-1 PageHeader, the chrome-widget release and the two-tier refresher. If ANY condition fails: make no code change, and file `gh issue create -R tusharsaxena/AbsorbTracker --label enhancement --label state:triaged --label severity:low --title 'Adopt O.RenderTabbedSchema opts for the Appearance page strip'` whose body names the failing condition and the re-check trigger (the next Options minor). The commit then carries only the A-04 comment below. A-04, either way: tests/test_widgets.lua :850 gets a comment that the strip is library-drawn (O.TabStrip / O.RenderTabbedSchema), so the wrap-invariance pin (band and row offsets identical for every tab selection) is LibKa0s's suite's and is not duplicated here (options-ui-§13 Testing, WS-06 (3), testing-§8).
- **Test first** Characterization first, green before and after an adoption, in tests/test_widgets.lua. For each unit in NS.Units.LIST and each tab: the strip's tab keys equal the schema group order; the rendered labels equal that group's rows; a mirrored unit (units.focus.mirror = true) renders the hint text and no appearance rows; ctx.__bannerHeight > 0 and ctx.chromeHeight >= ctx.__bannerHeight. The adoption must keep these green; the mutation 'disabledFor answers false' reddens the mirrored case. If the item declines, the characterization cases still land as the host pin of its own composition.

#### PM-01 — Schema degradation stub matches v1.56.0: SetMany, instance-id forwarding on Get/ApplyDefault, row.normalize

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PM, LK-22, PM-00
- **Findings** —
- **Change** PRE: RV-PM has landed, so libs/LibKa0s/ and tests/_kit/ are v1.56.0 / kit 26, and NS.SchemaRuntime (live, Schema minor 2) has SetMany. The stub is the only thing out of step: tests/test_surface_parity.lua 'Parity: the Schema seam's degraded surface matches the live one, on both levels' goes red naming SetMany. If RV-PM already put a SetMany member on the stub to keep its commit green, this item keeps only the behavior below and the cases.  settings/Schema.lua, hostSchemaStub() -> stubLib.New(_, d). Mirror LK-22 without the log or the tally. (1) Extract a local `prepare(row, path, value, id)`. It resolves `stored` / target(path, id) as R.Set does today, runs row.validate(value, rid) (refusal: `false, "invalid value", why`), and then runs `row.normalize(value, rid)` when present: the returned value replaces the input, and `nil, why` refuses with `false, "invalid value", why`. It checks nowhere-to-store and returns a plan table {row, parts, root, first, rid, value}. (2) Add a local `commit(plan)`: row.set(value), or stubLib.Write(root, parts, copy(value), first); then row.onChange(value, rid). (3) R.Set(path, value, id): find the row (unknown path answers `false, "unknown path: "..path`), then prepare, commit, d.announce, and return true. The observable order is unchanged. (4) New R.SetMany(entries, opts). opts = opts or {}. Phase 1: for each entry i, find the row; an unknown path returns `false, "unknown path: "..tostring(e.path), nil, i`. Run prepare with opts.instanceId, and on refusal return `false, err, why, i`. Nothing is stored in phase 1. Phase 2: when opts.act is set, R.BulkBegin() ... R.BulkEnd() (depth only), and commit each plan in order. Then, if d.announceBatch is a function, call it once with an array of {row=, path=, value=} and rid. Otherwise call d.announce per write. Return true. (5) R.Get(path, id) calls row.get(id). R.ApplyDefault(row, id) forwards id to R.Set(row.path, copy(row.default), id) (LK-22 R-14 parity). Every stub function stays at CCN 10 or below. Update the stub header comment ('write-completing and log-silent') to name SetMany and normalize. Also fix any other PanelMaster red the LK-05/LK-06/LK-07/LK-33 dry-runs recorded (<scratchpad>/dryrun-*-PanelMaster.txt) that RV-PM left. Give the setup what production does; never weaken an assertion.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_schema.lua (645 lines), on a degraded arm built with tests/degraded_env.lua loadPartial({ Schema = true }) and on the live NS.SchemaRuntime. (a) 'Schema stub: SetMany stores every entry in order and runs each onChange': two rows, settings.gridSize and settings.snapToGrid. (b) 'Schema stub: SetMany is all-or-nothing': entry 2 is invalid (gridSize 999). Expect `false, "invalid value", why, 2`, and entry 1 is still its old value. (c) 'Schema stub: SetMany with opts.act runs inside one bracket': R.InBulk() is true from inside an onChange and false after. (d) 'Schema: the live runtime and the stub answer SetMany identically': the same entries go to both arms, and the return tuples and stored values are compared. Before the stub change, (a)-(c) fail with 'attempt to call field SetMany', and the parity case is red. Add a '-- red under: delete R.SetMany from hostSchemaStub' comment.

#### PM-02 — Stand-down beats unlock: Canvas:Render strips the overlay while the latch is down, and the conformance suite gains the unlocked routes ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-PM, PM-00
- **Findings** PanelMaster-R-01, PanelMaster-R-02
- **Change** modules/Canvas.lua, Canvas:Render (around :786-825). Read the latch once, before applySpec: `local down = NS.Lifecycle and NS.Lifecycle:IsDown()`. Replace `if NS.Lifecycle and NS.Lifecycle:IsDown() then spec.shown = false end` with `if down then spec.shown = false end`. Replace the unconditional `if NS.Unlock and NS.Unlock.Decorate then NS.Unlock:Decorate(f, rec) end` with the following, so a stood-down panel is hidden, mouse-transparent and not movable:  ```lua if down then   if NS.Unlock and NS.Unlock.StripOverlay then NS.Unlock:StripOverlay(f) end elseif NS.Unlock and NS.Unlock.Decorate then   NS.Unlock:Decorate(f, rec) end ```  Leave NS.State.unlocked and NS.State.unlockedPanels untouched. NS.StandUp -> RenderAll then re-decorates from current state (performance-§6). Add no imperative Hide or force-lock in NS.StandDown (anti-pattern #85). Update the stand-down rung comment above it to say that the overlay is also stripped at this one source, and why Decorate's own f:Show() cannot override the latch. Update the header note at Canvas.lua:20-24, which names the two method-presence guards, to cover both branches.  docs/smoke-tests.md §7 Enable / disable: add the step 'Unlock, then /pm disable: no outline, no panel, no drag. Untick Lock frame while disabled: still nothing. /pm enable: the outlines come back.'
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_disabled.lua (580 lines). Add 'Disabled 5b: an unlocked panel is hidden, stripped and undraggable while stood down (routes A, B, C)', which uses seed()/shownPanels()/cleanup(). - Route A: NS.Unlock:SetUnlocked(true), then S:Set(ENABLED, false). - Route B: S:Set(ENABLED, false), then S:Set('state.locked', false), and again via NS.Slash:OnSlash('set state.locked false'). - Route C: disabled, then NS.Unlock:SetPanelUnlocked(id, true). - After each route: `#shownPanels() == 0`, and for every record `NS.Canvas:FrameFor(id):IsMouseEnabled() == false` (the wow_mock __mouse flag). - Positive half: S:Set(ENABLED, true). Both panels are shown with the mouse enabled. - Cleanup with SetUnlocked(false).  Add '-- red under: call NS.Unlock:Decorate unconditionally in Canvas:Render (modules/Canvas.lua)'. Run the suite before the Canvas change and watch A, B and C fail on shown=2/1.
- **Verify** All commands run under ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded: - `luacheck .` gives 0 warnings / 0 errors. - `lua5.1 tests/run.lua` gives 0 failed. - `lizard -l lua -x "./libs/*" -x "./tests/_kit/*" -w -C 15 .` prints nothing; Canvas:Render stays at CCN 15 or below. - The 1500-line check prints nothing. - docs/test-cases.md is regenerated with `lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list`, and the README badge is bumped in the same commit.

Then set __inCombat = true, run the fight, set __inCombat = false, and call OnRegenEnabled: the panel is shown. #### PM-05 — RenderAll releases every mismatched frame before rendering, so a profile swap of frame names never creates a duplicate named frame ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-PM, PM-03, PM-00
- **Findings** PanelMaster-R-05
- **Change** modules/Canvas.lua Canvas:RenderAll(inCombat) (:829-842, keeping PM-03's parameter). Make it two passes: 1. Build `want[id] = NS.Registry.FrameName(rec)` for every record in NS.Registry:All(). Use a file-local scratch table that is wiped at the top of each call, so RenderAll allocates nothing per call. 2. For every `id, f in pairs(active)` where `want[id] ~= f.__frameName` (a retired id or a changed name): release(f); active[id] = nil. 3. Canvas:Render(rec.id, inCombat) for each record. Keep Render(id)'s single-id mismatch branch (:811-820) as the fallback for the per-id MSG_PANEL path, and say so in its comment. Put the release pass in a local helper (releaseMismatched) so RenderAll stays at a small CCN. Update the pool comment (:25-40): the one-frame-per-name bound now holds across a profile switch that swaps names between ids, because the release pass runs before any acquire.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_profiles.lua: 'Profiles: swapping frame names across ids creates no second named frame'. 1. Seed profile P1 with {1 = Alpha, 2 = Xray} and P2 with {1 = Xray, 2 = Alpha}, through the mock AceDB profiles. 2. Switch between them six times via NS.db:SetProfile (fires OnProfileChanged). 3. Wrap the mock's CreateFrame inside the case to count calls whose name argument is non-nil, and restore it afterwards. 4. Assert the count is 0 after the first render. 5. Assert `_G[name] == NS.Canvas:FrameFor(id)` for both ids after every switch. 6. Assert NS.Canvas.PooledCount() does not grow. Before the fix, the count is 6 (red).
- **Verify** All commands run under ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded: - `luacheck .` is clean. - `lua5.1 tests/run.lua` gives 0 failed, including tests/test_disabled.lua 'Disabled 5' and the existing test_profiles cases. - lizard: RenderAll and its helper are at CCN 15 or below. - The 1500-line check prints nothing. - test-cases.md and the badge are regenerated.
- **Smoke** In client (docs/smoke-tests.md §12): two profiles whose panels carry swapped names. Switch back and forth five times, then run /pm debug dump. The 'frames: N active, M pooled, 0 orphaned' line has no orphans, and /framestack over each panel names the expected PanelMaster_Panel_<slug>.

Live arm, at column zero so tests/test_surface_parity.lua's Core derivation (`\nNS%.([A-Za-z_]+)%s*=`) picks it up: `NS.SafeRegisterEvent = lib.SafeRegisterEvent` (LibKa0s-Core-1.0 minor 8, LK-11). Degraded arm, indented inside `if not lib then`: a one-rung body with no front gate:  ```lua function NS.SafeRegisterEvent(target, event, handler, rejected)   local ok = pcall(target.RegisterEvent, target, event, handler)   if not ok and type(rejected) == "table" then     -- append event once (no duplicates)   end   return ok end ```  Publish only SafeRegisterEvent. This seam's stated rule is that its surface is exactly what the addon calls. PanelMaster registers no unit events and no event arrays, so SafeRegisterUnitEvent and SafeRegisterEvents are not re-exported (note this beside the assignment). #### PM-09 — Library-absent composed-row verbs follow WS-02: enable/disable write through Schema writeThrough; lock/unlock print the library-absent line; the stub's DisabledLine is pinned to the library constant ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PM, PM-01, LK-23, LK-18, WS-02, PM-00
- **Findings** PanelMaster-R-09
- **Change** Implements WS-02 route (a) for enable/disable (the SHOULD) and route (b) for lock/unlock. The composed Master-controls rows are absent whenever the Options major is: on a partial load where only Options is missing, and on a fully library-less load. (1) settings/Schema.lua. Pass `writeThrough = { S.ENABLED_PATH }` on the SchemaLib:New descriptor (Schema minor 2, LK-23). In hostSchemaStub's R.Set, when FindRow misses and the path is in the descriptor's writeThrough set (built once in New), store a copy raw via target()/stubLib.Write with no validate, normalize or onChange. Then call d.announce with a synthetic row `{ path = path, writeThrough = true }` built once per path, and return true. Every other row-less path is still refused ('unknown path: ...'). Update the stub header comment. (2) settings/Slash.lua, the shared part above the `if not lib` branch. Add `function Sl:CliEnable(on)`: - If NS.Schema:FindRow(S.ENABLED_PATH) finds the row, `return Sl:CliSet(S.ENABLED_PATH .. " " .. tostring(on))`. This is the live path, byte-identical to today. - Else, if NS.Schema:Set(S.ENABLED_PATH, on) succeeds, call NS.RefreshEnabled() (writeThrough skips onChange, so the latch is re-evaluated here), then print Sl.FormatKV(path, tostring(NS.Schema:Get(path))). - Else print Sl:LibraryAbsentLine('/pm ' .. (on and 'enable' or 'disable')). Add `function Sl:CliLock(locked)`: a row present means CliSet('state.locked ' .. tostring(locked)); otherwise print Sl:LibraryAbsentLine('/pm ' .. (locked and 'lock' or 'unlock')) with no write and no error. `Sl:LibraryAbsentLine(verb)` returns `NS.L["%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load."]:format(verb)`, the slash-commands-§1 line (WS-02). NS.COMMANDS enable/disable/unlock/lock call these four handlers. Fix the stale comment at :292-293 in the same edit: the enabled row's onChange is NS.RefreshEnabled() and it does not announce. (3) The Slash degradation arm, per LK-18's prescribed stub shape (docs/api/Slash/version-15-docs.md 'The degradation stub'): - Publish `Sl.DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT` on BOTH arms: live `= lib.DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT`; stub = the existing literal. Sl:DisabledLine() on the stub formats from it. It is a string, so the Slash parity case's function-only extra-member check is unaffected. - The degraded Sl.PrintHelp prints the UNAVAILABLE line once, then one `/pm <cmd>  <desc>` row per NS.COMMANDS entry, the `cmd  desc` shape with no FormatRow copy. (4) locales/enUS.lua: add the key, with a comment saying it is the collection's library-absent line and routes through the locale per slash-commands-§1. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md: re-word the localization-§1 register row: one string, the library-absent line, now routes through NS.L; everything else is still English-only. docs/slash-dispatch.md: a 'Library-absent load' paragraph.
- **Test first** Red first.  tests/test_libka0s.lua, fully degraded (Env.loadDegraded()) and partial (Env.loadPartial({ Options = true, OptionsWidgets = true, OptionsScroll = true, OptionsCompose = true })): - 'Degraded install: /pm disable and /pm enable write through and flip the latch without raising'. Run `pcall(ns.Slash.OnSlash, ns.Slash, 'disable')`: ok is true, ns.db.profile.settings.enabled is false, ns.Lifecycle:IsDown() is true, and the last chat line is PREFIX .. FormatKV('settings.enabled', 'false'). Then enable and assert the reverse. - 'Degraded install: /pm unlock and /pm lock print the library-absent line and change nothing': exactly one line, `NS.L[...]:format('/pm unlock')`; NS.State.unlocked is unchanged; no error. - 'Degraded install: the help index still lists every verb'. - On the live arm, the tests/test_slash.lua:741-744 echo cases stay unchanged.  tests/test_surface_parity.lua, in the Slash case: `assertLibraryConstant(Sl.DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT, 'LibKa0s-Slash-1.0', 'DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT')`, where Sl is the degraded arm (kit 26 LK-18). Add `['LibKa0s-Slash-1.0'] = mocks.LibStub('LibKa0s-Slash-1.0')` to tests/run.lua's Kit.setSurfaceSource map if the kit's name resolution needs it.  tests/test_schema.lua: - 'Schema stub: a writeThrough path is stored without a row; any other row-less path is refused'. - The same on the live instance on the partial load.  Before the change, the degraded /pm disable prints the UNAVAILABLE text and the latch stays up (red).
- **Verify** All commands run under ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded: - `luacheck .` is clean. - `lua5.1 tests/run.lua` gives 0 failed: the Slash, Schema and Options parity cases, the 'L trap' cases in tests/test_libka0s.lua (no descriptor receives NS.L), and 'Degraded install: the schema loses the composed Master controls rows and NOTHING else' (the row count is unchanged; writeThrough adds no row). - lizard: CliEnable, CliLock and the stub R.Set are at CCN 10 or below. - The 1500-line check prints nothing. - test-cases.md and the badge are regenerated.
- **Smoke** Optional, in client: temporarily rename libs/LibKa0s. /pm disable then /pm enable. The panels go and come back, the chat shows the echo, and there is no Lua error. /pm unlock prints '/pm unlock is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.' Restore the folder.

#### PM-10 — Migration stamp per WS-03: defaults declare schemaVersion = 0, the v1->v2 step walks every stored profile, and the docs stop claiming the stamp is undeclared

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PM, WS-03, PM-00
- **Findings** PanelMaster-A-05, PanelMaster-A-06, PanelMaster-A-17, PanelMaster-R-09
- **Change** (1) defaults/Global.lua: `NS.defaults.global = { schemaVersion = 0, minimap = { hide = false } }`. Replace the 'DELIBERATELY NOT SEEDED' block (:8-20) with the v2.65.0 reason (savedvariables-§1). 0 masks no legacy account, because an unstamped account reads 0 and the runner opens its gate. A current-version default would be stripped by removeDefaults and never persist. The runner owns the stamp. (2) core/Database.lua NS:RunMigrations (:153-186): - `local from = tonumber(g.schemaVersion) or 0`. - Extract `local function stampFrameNames(profile) ... return rows end`. - The v1->v2 step (from < 2) walks EVERY stored profile: `for _, prof in pairs(NS.db.sv and NS.db.sv.profiles or {}) do ... end`, plus NS.db.profile if it is not among them (identity check). The account-wide stamp alone no longer gates a profile-scoped step. - The stamp is written only after the step returns without raising. Update the RegisterProfileCallbacks comment (:95-103): inactive profiles are now stamped at init, and R.Sanitize stays as the per-record repair for imported profiles. Fix the stale sentences R-09 names in these files: core/Database.lua:16 ('global holds only the schema stamp' becomes 'global holds the schemaVersion stamp and LibDBIcon's minimap table'), the :170 example ('schema v1' becomes 'schema v2'), and defaults/Profile.lua:4-5. (3) Docs (A-06). docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:34, docs/common-tasks.md:12-13, docs/module-map.md:24 and docs/profiles.md:11 each say that defaults/Global.lua declares schemaVersion = 0 (the runner's floor; the runner writes the real stamp) and LibDBIcon's minimap table. docs/schema.md:21 is left as is. Add no Documented-deviations row: declaring 0 is now compliance with toc-file-§2 and savedvariables-§1 (v2.65.0), which closes A-05 and A-17.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_database.lua: - 'Database: defaults declare schemaVersion 0': NS.defaults.global.schemaVersion == 0. - 'Database.RunMigrations: a legacy record in a NON-active stored profile gets its frame name': seed db.sv.profiles.Other.panels = {{ id = 1, name = 'Chat BG' }} with no frameName, reset db.global.schemaVersion to nil (reads the 0 default), run NS:RunMigrations(), and assert db.sv.profiles.Other.panels[1].frameName == NS.Util.FrameName('Chat BG'). - 'Database.RunMigrations: the stamp lands above the declared default': db.global.schemaVersion == NS.SCHEMA_VERSION. - Update 'stamps a version onto an unstamped DB' and the MigrationSummary expectation to 'v0 -> v2'. Before the change, the inactive profile stays unstamped (red).
- **Verify** All commands run under ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded: - `luacheck .` is clean. - `lua5.1 tests/run.lua` gives 0 failed; `S:Register()` returns 0. - `grep -rn 'only the schema stamp\\|carries only the\\|schemaVersion stamp — the one' docs core defaults` prints nothing outside frozen bundles. - lizard: RunMigrations at CCN 10 or below. - The 1500-line check prints nothing. - test-cases.md and the badge are regenerated.

#### PM-11 — Minimap row CLI path reads in its shown sense: global.minimap.shown, stored at minimap.hide ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-PM, WS-06, PM-00
- **Findings** —
- **Change** settings/Schema.lua: - :187: S.MINIMAP_PATH = "global.minimap.shown". - Add S.MINIMAP_STORE = "global.minimap.hide", LibDBIcon's key and the only stored key. - The wired row (:530-539): get = `not SchemaLib.Read(NS.db, S.MINIMAP_STORE)`; set writes `SchemaLib.Write(NS.db, S.MINIMAP_STORE, not v)`, then calls NS.Launcher:SetShown(v). - S:SnapshotPersisted keeps excluding the row by S.MINIMAP_PATH. - S:Register: the defaultsRoot callback receives (parts, row). Return nil for `row and row.path == S.MINIMAP_PATH`: the row owns its storage and is checked by its store. After R.Validate, add one missing count, with a printed schema error, if `SchemaLib.Read(NS.defaults, S.MINIMAP_STORE) == nil`. - Rewrite the comments at :170-186 and :516-529 per launcher-§3 (v2.65.0, WS-06). The row's path is the CLI name and reads SHOWN; the store stays hide; no `shown` key is ever written (anti-pattern #81). core/LauncherSetup.lua:147: the comment names S.MINIMAP_STORE. Docs: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:41, :201 and :215 and docs/schema.md:48 say `/pm get global.minimap.shown`, stored at db.global.minimap.hide. No SavedVariables migration is needed.  Minimap rename carry-over (WS-06): the stored LibDBIcon key db.global.minimap.hide does NOT move, so every existing player keeps their setting with no SavedVariables migration and no schema-version bump (a stored `shown` key would violate anti-pattern #81). Pin it with a test written first: a legacy store with minimap = { hide = true, minimapPos = 200 } reads `<slash> get <path>.minimap.shown` as false, the button stays hidden, minimapPos is untouched, and no `shown` key is ever written to the raw SV after a set. Record the player-facing CLI rename (`...minimap.hide` -> `...minimap.shown`, old path answers unknown setting) in the commit body for the next Version History roll.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_launcher.lua (:207-378). - Change the pin `assertEqual(S.MINIMAP_PATH, "global.minimap.hide")` to "global.minimap.shown". - Add 'Launcher: /pm get global.minimap.shown answers true while the button shows, and writes land on hide': `NS.Slash:OnSlash('get global.minimap.shown')` echoes 'true' when hide = false. `OnSlash('set global.minimap.shown false')` sets db.global.minimap.hide == true and db.global.minimap.shown == nil. - `S:Register()` returns 0. - Keep every hide-side assertion, which now reads through the store. Before the change, the path pin and the get echo are red.
- **Verify** All commands run under ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded: - `luacheck .` is clean. - `lua5.1 tests/run.lua` gives 0 failed, including both Defaults-survival cases in test_launcher. - `grep -rn 'global.minimap.hide' settings core docs/*.md` shows only the MINIMAP_STORE definition and docs that name the store. - lizard: no function above CCN 15. - The 1500-line check prints nothing. - test-cases.md and the badge are regenerated.
- **Smoke** In client (docs/smoke-tests.md §7b): - /pm get global.minimap.shown prints true. - /pm set global.minimap.shown false: the button goes. - LibDBIcon's right-click hide, then /pm get global.minimap.shown: prints false. - /reload: the button stays hidden.

#### PC-03 — Migration runner: profile-scoped steps run on every stored profile, and the stamp advances only past a step that succeeded (WS-03)

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PC, PC-00
- **Findings** PRETTYCHAT-R-03
- **Change** core/Database.lua (:41-115). The migrations table becomes `migrations[v] = { scope = 'profile' \| 'global', run = function(target, db, profileName) end }`, published as `Database.migrations` so a suite can inject steps on a fresh instance. It is still empty after this item. - New `local function runProfileStep(db, step)`. It walks the RAW SavedVariables profiles, `db.sv and db.sv.profiles` (the kit AceDB fake and real AceDB both expose db.sv), and pcalls step.run(profileTable, db, name) for every table-valued profile. It falls back to db.profile only when db.sv is absent. It returns false, err on the first raise. - runSteps(db, from) returns ran and `reached`. For v = from+1..SCHEMA_VERSION, a present step runs: profile scope through runProfileStep, global scope through pcall(step.run, db.global, db). On failure it prints the existing 'schema migration v failed: <err>' line and STOPS, returning the last v that completed. A missing step advances reached. - RunMigrations stamps `db.global.schemaVersion = reached` when from < SCHEMA_VERSION. It keeps normalizing a stamp that is already ahead to SCHEMA_VERSION, which the pinned case tests/test_database.lua:59 requires. - The [Migrate] trace reports from->reached. - Rewrite the header (:3-18) and the :78-97 comments. SCHEMA_VERSION is the runner's target. The runner owns the stamp. `global.schemaVersion = 0` is the declared default (already true at :36) and it survives AceDB's removeDefaults. Every step is idempotent against a fresh default profile. Rewrite the core/PrettyChat.lua:54-56 comment. Profiles are lifted at load across every stored profile, so a copied profile is already current, and the re-run in the profile callbacks is an idempotent no-op plus the orphan repair. Keep RunMigrations and runSteps at CCN 10 or below; split helpers if needed.
- **Test first** tests/test_database.lua. Each case uses a fresh ctx.loadAddon and injects into NS.Database.migrations with a test-local SCHEMA_VERSION, restored at the end. Mark each '-- red under: runSteps calling step(db) once and RunMigrations stamping unconditionally'. (a) 'a profile-scoped step lifts every stored profile, not only the active one'. The SV holds profiles Default and Alt, each with a marker, at global.schemaVersion = 1. A v2 profile step renames the marker. Assert both raw profiles are lifted, and SetProfile('Alt') reads the lifted shape. (b) 'a raising step leaves the stamp where it was'. A v2 global step raises. The stamp stays 1 and one 'schema migration 2 failed' line is printed. (c) 'a step after a failed one does not run'. (d) 'a profile step runs again safely on an already-lifted profile' (idempotence). Existing cases :10-157 stay green. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the README Tests badge.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/PrettyChat && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && diff --strip-trailing-cr <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list) docs/test-cases.md && lizard -l lua -x './libs/*' -x './tests/_kit/*' -C 15 -w . && git ls-files '*.lua' \| grep -v '^libs/\\|^tests/_kit/\\|^GlobalStrings/GlobalStrings.lua$' \| xargs wc -l \| sort -rn \| sed -n 2p  # must be < 1500. No tests/perf.lua (performance-§12). `lizard -l lua core/Database.lua` shows RunMigrations and runSteps at CCN 10 or below.

#### PC-04 — Drop the dead Loot copies of LOOT_ITEM_CREATED_SELF/_MULTIPLE, with an idempotent v2 profile migration onto Tradeskill; no global registered twice ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PC, PC-03, PC-00 · **Issues** PrettyChat#4

(2) core/Database.lua: SCHEMA_VERSION 1 -> 2, plus `migrations[2] = { scope = 'profile', run = liftLootCreatedSelf }`. For each of the two globals, the step does this: - If cats.Loot.strings[G] is set and cats.Tradeskill.strings[G] is nil, it copies the value there, unless the value equals NS.Defaults.Tradeskill.strings[G].default, which setters store as absence. NS.Defaults is loaded by OnInitialize time. - It clears cats.Loot.strings[G] and cats.Loot.disabledStrings[G]. - It prunes an emptied strings or disabledStrings table and an emptied Loot table. The step is idempotent. The runner's existing v1->v2 [Migrate] line is the one log line. (3) settings/Schema.lua: delete the Schema.crossRegisteredGlobals block and its stale comment (:446-468). #### PC-05 — Move Schema.ResetRows onto the schema runtime's BulkRun/BulkAdd bracket (issue #18) ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PC, PC-01, PC-00 · **Issues** PrettyChat#18
- **Findings** —
- **Change** settings/Schema.lua, resetRowsBody (:932-956) and Schema.ResetRows (:958-968). (1) Filter first. Build `eligible` from rows that pass `S.FindRow(row.path) == row and not refusedBySignature(row, row.default)`. If it is empty, return 0 without opening a bracket, so an act with no eligible row still logs nothing, as today. (2) Run `S.BulkRun('reset', label, walk)` (NS.SchemaRuntime). act = 'reset' and scope = label keep the console line byte-identical, `[Set] reset <label>: N rows`, and the library's STOPPED suffix equals NS.Util.STOPPED. (3) In walk, for each eligible row: `local before = row.get(); PrettyChat.Batch(function() row.set(row.default) end); if row.get() ~= before then S.BulkAdd(1); n = n + 1 end`. That is a read-back count, the runtime's own semantic. (4) Keep the reapply and category tracking. The single `PrettyChat:ApplyStrings()` (when reapply) and the single `Schema.NotifyPanelChange(category or nil)` stay INSIDE walk, so a raise in either is stopped-marked, as today. (5) Delete the `{wrote, changed}` tally, the line() closure and the NS.Util.RunAct call. Delete differsFromDefault if nothing else reads it. (6) Keep returning n if `grep -n 'ResetRows(' modules settings tests` shows a reader of the return value; otherwise drop the return. (7) Rewrite the comments at :57-60 and :880-931, and any RunAct mention for ResetRows in docs/schema.md, docs/ARCHITECTURE.md and docs/module-map.md. The degraded stub's BulkRun (:700-705) already brackets and re-raises. It logs nothing, which matches the degraded NS.Debug, a no-op in core/DebugLogSetup.lua:70. The re-raise now comes from the library's `error(err, 0)` after a plain pcall, not Util.RunAct's xpcall with a traceback. If a test_override case matches on stack text, assert the message only and say so in the commit body.
- **Test first** Characterization pass before the code change; issue #18 asks for one. - Record green runs of the existing bulk-line pins in tests/test_override.lua (:433-470: the stopped-line wording and count for a row set raising mid-batch and for the re-apply raising) and of the one-line reset cases (:244, :353). - Add 'an all-already-default reset still logs `[Set] reset Loot: 0 rows`'. - Add 'a reset list with no eligible row logs nothing and returns 0'. - Red case: 'ResetRows runs inside the runtime's bracket'. Spy NS.SchemaRuntime.BulkRun and assert one call with ('reset', 'Loot') on addon:ResetCategory('Loot'). It is red under RunAct. - Add a read-back count case: General.enabled stored false, then ResetCategory('General'), counts 1 even though its set stores nil. All existing pins stay green UNCHANGED (the issue's Done-when). Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the README Tests badge.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/PrettyChat && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && diff --strip-trailing-cr <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list) docs/test-cases.md && lizard -l lua -x './libs/*' -x './tests/_kit/*' -C 15 -w . && git ls-files '*.lua' \| grep -v '^libs/\\|^tests/_kit/\\|^GlobalStrings/GlobalStrings.lua$' \| xargs wc -l \| sort -rn \| sed -n 2p  # must be < 1500. No tests/perf.lua. `grep -n 'RunAct' settings/Schema.lua` no longer shows ResetRows, and `grep -n 'BulkRun\\|BulkAdd' settings/Schema.lua` does.

#### PC-11 — One memoized sorted name list per category (NS.SortedStringNames) replaces four rebuild-and-sort loops ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-PC, PC-04, PC-00
- **Findings** PRETTYCHAT-R-09
- **Change** modules/Override.lua, near the top after LABEL: add the cache `local sortedNames = {}` and `function NS.SortedStringNames(category)`. On first call it builds the sorted global-name array from NS.Defaults[category].strings (or {}) and caches it. It returns the cached array, with a '-- READ-ONLY: callers must not mutate' comment. NS.Defaults is static after load; there are no runtime writes. Replace the four sites: - ApplyStrings (:283-287) iterates NS.SortedStringNames(category). - collectNames (:566-575) filters INTO A NEW TABLE from the cached array and never filters the cache. - settings/Schema.lua:434-438 (the schema build loop). - settings/Panel.lua:454-458. The rule_ref is none: this is a maintainability and perf tidy-up, and the perf benefit is unmeasured (there is no tests/perf.lua, by exemption). docs/module-map.md gets a row for NS.SortedStringNames.
- **Test first** Characterization first, green before and after: - tests/test_override.lua 'the /pc test report lists each category's strings in sorted order'. Capture PrettyChat:Test(nil, sink) and assert that the Name: sequence per category equals the sorted keys of NS.Defaults. #### PC-12 — The combat watcher registers through Core's SafeRegisterEvents with a rejected-name record, and the event/bus docs cite the v2.65.0 carve-out and threshold ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-PC, PC-01, LK-11, PC-00
- **Findings** PRETTYCHAT-A-09, PRETTYCHAT-A-08, PRETTYCHAT-A-11
- **Change** (1) modules/Override.lua. - Publish `NS.RejectedEvents = NS.RejectedEvents or {}` and `local WATCH_EVENTS = { "PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED", "PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED" }`. - In SyncCombatWatch, replace the bare loop (:138-140) with `local n = NS.Util.SafeRegisterEvents(combatWatcher, WATCH_EVENTS, nil, NS.RejectedEvents)`. When n < #WATCH_EVENTS, write one `NS.Debug("Events", "rejected %s", table.concat(NS.RejectedEvents, ", "))` line. - The helper is LibKa0s-Core's SafeRegisterEvents: the C_EventUtils.IsEventValid front gate, then a pcall. Its degraded body comes from PC-01. - Rewrite the :83-96 comment. The frame is events-frames-taint-§1's no-AceEvent carve-out (v2.65.0, WS-04): one lazily created private watcher for non-unit boundary events, fully unregistered on stand-down, and listed in ## Event Subscriptions with no register row. (2) core/DebugLogSetup.lua sessionSummary (:22-37): when #NS.RejectedEvents > 0, append `, rejected events: <names>`. This surfaces the list in the [Init] summary. The summary is unchanged when the list is empty, so the test_debuglog shape pins hold. PrettyChat has one feature module (modules/Override.lua), and no second feature module reacts to its events. The AceAddon shell's handlers are not a feature module. Keep the re-check trigger, reworded to 'a second feature module, or one that must react to the watcher's events' (A-11). (4) docs/module-map.md: add a row for NS.RejectedEvents.  Upstream follow-up LK-11: this item completes it. The modules/Override.lua SyncCombatWatch registration loop (:138-140) goes through Core.SafeRegisterEvents (NS.Util.SafeRegisterEvents, bound by PC-01), and the caller-owned rejected list is NS.RejectedEvents (PRETTYCHAT-A-09).

#### WG-05 — Replace modules/Frame.lua's two private PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED frames with a combat-end queue drained by the addon's AceEvent handler ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-WG, WG-03, WG-00
- **Findings** WHATGROUP-A-08
- **Change** modules/Frame.lua. (1) Add a file-scope queue `local combatEndQueue = {}` with two functions. `NS.FrameQueueForCombatEnd(key, fn)` stores `combatEndQueue[key] = fn`, one slot per key, so repeat queueing replaces rather than stacks. `NS.FrameDrainCombatEnd()` walks the fixed keys 'teleport' then 'firstShow', takes each fn, sets its slot to nil, then calls it. There is no table allocation when the queue is empty, which keeps perf combatGateSteady at 0 B. (2) deferTeleportUntilCombatEnds (:526-541): replace f:RegisterEvent/OnEvent with `f._pendingTeleportInfo = (info == nil) and NO_CAPTURE or info; NS.FrameQueueForCombatEnd('teleport', replayTeleport)`. replayTeleport is a file-scope function holding WG-03's replay body (latest-info semantics, NO_CAPTURE preserved). (3) ShowFrame's first-show defer (:1014-1040): delete `buildWaitFrame` (:232 and its creation). Queue 'firstShow' with a file-scope replay that clears `_frameBuildQueued`, restores `pendingInfo` from the stashed `WhatGroup._deferredShowInfo`, and calls ShowFrame. (4) NS.FrameStandDown (:1115-1123): replace the raw UnregisterEvent/SetScript calls with wiping the queue and clearing `f._pendingTeleportInfo`, `_deferredShowInfo` and `_frameBuildQueued`. core/WhatGroup.lua OnCombatStateChanged (:900-908): on 'PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED', call `NS.FrameDrainCombatEnd()` first, before ApplyFrameVisibility. OnDisabledCombatEnded (:352-355) does NOT drain. Rewrite the Frame.lua comments that describe the two raw registrations (:230-232, :999-1011, :1109-1114). #### WG-06 — Route the four event registrations through LibKa0s-Core's SafeRegisterEvent, with the rejected names in the [Init] summary ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-WG, WG-05, WG-00
- **Findings** WHATGROUP-A-07
- **Change** core/CoreSetup.lua live branch (after :108): `NS.SafeRegisterEvent = lib.SafeRegisterEvent` (Core minor 8, LK-11). Degraded branch (:40-99): a one-rung body with no front gate, as the Core version-8 doc's Degradation note prescribes: `function NS.SafeRegisterEvent(target, event, handler, rejected) local ok = pcall(target.RegisterEvent, target, event, handler); if not ok and rejected then <append event once, no duplicates> end; return ok end`. Only SafeRegisterEvent is published: WhatGroup registers no unit events and walks no list, and the Core parity case compares the namespace live against degraded, so an unpublished member needs no stub. Say so in a comment. core/WhatGroup.lua: add `NS.RejectedEvents = {}` next to NS.State (session-only, never persisted). registerFeatureEvents (:293-304) makes four calls: `NS.SafeRegisterEvent(self, 'GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE', nil, NS.RejectedEvents)`, and the same for LFG_LIST_APPLICATION_STATUS_UPDATED and for PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED/ENABLED with 'OnCombatStateChanged'. The rest of OnEnable (Settings.Register, Launcher:Register, the latch) then always runs. InitSummary (:419-433): when #NS.RejectedEvents > 0, append `, rejected events: <names joined by ', '>`. The string is unchanged when the list is empty. docs/midnight-quirks.md: one paragraph on the probing trade. C_EventUtils.IsEventValid is asked first when present, and a pcall catches a retail raise on an unknown name. The rejected list is the only record. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md '## Event Subscriptions' names the helper. Before this item, check that tests/test_surface_parity.lua's namespace case is green on both arms.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_harness.lua. 'events: one retired event name does not abort OnEnable' seeds `mock.__badEvents = { GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE = true }` through the enableAddon mock hook. It asserts the other three are in NS.addon.__events, `#mock.categories == 2`, `NS.Launcher:IsRegistered()`, `NS.Lifecycle` evaluated (IsDown false), `NS.RejectedEvents` equals {'GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE'}, and NS.addon:InitSummary() contains 'rejected events: GROUP_ROSTER_UPDATE'. It carries '-- red under: a bare self:RegisterEvent in registerFeatureEvents'; confirm it is red first (OnEnable raises). Variant with `mock.C_EventUtils = nil` (the pcall rung). Variant 'degraded: the Core stub's SafeRegisterEvent survives a bad name' with skip = NO_LIBKA0S. 'events: a stand-up after a rejection records the name once'. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.

#### WG-08 — Declare global.schemaVersion = 0 so the stamp persists, and make the migration runner step-driven (savedvariables-§1 at v2.65.0) ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-WG, WS-03, WG-00
- **Findings** WHATGROUP-R-03
- **Change** settings/Schema.lua:609 BuildDefaults: `global = { schemaVersion = 0, windows = {}, minimap = { hide = false } }`. Rewrite the :596-603 comment. AceDB's removeDefaults strips a stored value equal to its default at logout, so a default equal to the current version never persists and the first real migration is skipped (WS-03). core/Database.lua: keep `NS.SCHEMA_VERSION = 1` and add `NS.MIGRATIONS = { [1] = function(db) end }`. Step N takes the store from N-1 to N, and 0 -> 1 is the no-op stamp. RunMigrations: `local g = self.db and self.db.global; if not g then return end; local from = g.schemaVersion or 0; local v = from; while v < NS.SCHEMA_VERSION do local step = NS.MIGRATIONS[v + 1]; if step then step(self.db) end; v = v + 1; g.schemaVersion = v end`. The stamp advances only past a step that returned, and a raising step propagates. Delete the dead `g.schemaVersion = g.schemaVersion or NS.SCHEMA_VERSION`. The Migrate debug line becomes `NS.Debug('Migrate', 'v%s -> v%s', from, g.schemaVersion)`, the format form (the core/Database.lua:43 site of WHATGROUP-R-05). Header comment: steps are idempotent against a fresh default profile. WhatGroup has no profile-scoped step today; the first one MUST walk every stored profile (raw WhatGroupDB.profiles), per WS-03. docs/schema.md: the global block shows schemaVersion = 0, and a paragraph explains why.
- **Test first** tests/test_database.lua. Rewrite :27 'BuildDefaults seeds global.schemaVersion from NS.SCHEMA_VERSION' as 'database: defaults declare global.schemaVersion 0 (savedvariables-§1)', asserting 0. Add the red case 'database: the stamp survives AceDB's logout strip, so the first real migration runs'. Boot. Mirror removeDefaults on the raw SV: delete global.schemaVersion when it equals the declared default. Re-boot from that SV with `NS.SCHEMA_VERSION = 2` and `NS.MIGRATIONS[2] = function(db) db.global.__stepRan = true end` set before OnInitialize (a loader mock hook). Assert __stepRan is true and schemaVersion == 2. It carries '-- red under: the default equal to NS.SCHEMA_VERSION', and must fail on the current code, where the stamp is stripped and the default reads 2. Add 'database: a raising step leaves the stamp at the last completed version'. Keep 'fresh DB lands at schemaVersion 1' green; the runner stamps it. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/WhatGroup && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && diff <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list) docs/test-cases.md && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua && lizard -l lua -x './libs/*' -x './tests/_kit/*' -C 15 -w . && git ls-files '*.lua' \| grep -v '^libs/' \| grep -v '^tests/_kit/' \| xargs wc -l \| sort -n \| tail -4 && ! grep -n 'or NS.SCHEMA_VERSION' core/Database.lua settings/Schema.lua
- **Smoke** S-003 in client: /reload, log out, and inspect WTF/Account/<acct>/SavedVariables/WhatGroup.lua. WhatGroupDB.global.schemaVersion = 1 is present.

#### WG-12 — Adopt LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 (WhatGroup#22); on a library-absent load /wg enable, /wg disable and /wg test print the library-absent line and raise no Lua error ⚠

- **Effort** L · **Depends on** RV-WG, WG-08, WG-09, WG-11, LK-23, WG-00 · **Issues** WhatGroup#22
- **Findings** —
- **Change** Follows the seam-adopter map in the 2026-09-22 schema spec §11 ('WhatGroup — seam adopter') and the Schema version-2 doc. (1) NEW settings/SchemaSetup.lua. Add it to WhatGroup.toc directly above settings\Schema.lua with a load-bearing comment: Schema.lua calls its :New at file scope. It resolves `LibStub and LibStub('LibKa0s-Schema-1.0', true)`, or else HostSchemaStub, and publishes it as `WhatGroup.Settings.SchemaLib`. HostSchemaStub is the write-completing, log-silent stub copied from LibKa0s tests/test_schema.lua `referenceStub` at v1.56.0 and trimmed to what WhatGroup calls. Library level: SplitPath, Read, Write, SameValue, New. Instance: AllRows, AddRows, FindRow, Reindex, Get, Set (unknown path refused and never stored, then validate, missing root, store a copy, onChange, announce, true), SetMany (all-or-nothing: resolve and validate every entry, then store in order, with no log and one announce each), Default, ApplyDefault (resetExempt vetoed only while a bracket is open), a depth-only BulkBegin/BulkEnd/InBulk/BulkRun, BulkAdd no-op, CountOffDefault 0, ResetCounted running fn, ConsumeResetCount nil, and Validate returning 0,0,0 with one honest line. Comment: a deliberate duplication named by 'docs/api/Schema/version-2-docs.md, The degradation stub'. No writeThrough list is passed: route (b) is taken, per the deviation row below. (2) settings/Schema.lua. Delete Resolve (:288-305), the SESSION/GLOBAL dispatch inside Helpers.Get/RawSet (:388-419), Helpers.RawSet, sameValue, the bulk state and Bulk table (:435-527), silenceOpenBracket, FindSchema, countChangedProfileRows, pendingResetCount and Settings.ConsumeResetCount's body. Build `local S = Settings.SchemaLib:New{ rows = Schema, resolveRoot = function() return WhatGroup.db and WhatGroup.db.profile, 1 end, announce = function() Helpers.RefreshAll() end, debug = function(tag, fmt, ...) NS.Debug(tag, fmt, ...) end, debugEnabled = function() return NS.State.debug == true end, print = pout, resetExempt = { [MINIMAP_PATH] = true } }` and set `NS.SchemaRuntime = S`. Bind `Helpers.Get, Helpers.Set, Helpers.FindSchema, Helpers.ApplyDefault = S.Get, S.Set, S.FindRow, S.ApplyDefault`, `Settings.Bulk = { begin = S.BulkBegin, finish = S.BulkEnd }` and `Settings.ConsumeResetCount = S.ConsumeResetCount`. Add `Settings.StampClosureRows(rows)`: on each row whose path is in SESSION or GLOBAL, stamp `row.get` (session: `spec.get() or false`, as today) and `row.set` (the SESSION spec's set, or GLOBAL's inverting set). Helpers.ValidateSchema = `S.Validate{ types = { bool = true, number = true, string = true } }` plus the host loop for section and label (single-consumer checks), with errors summed. RestoreAllDefaults: `S.ResetCounted(function() db:ResetProfile() end, function(row) return not GLOBAL[row.path] end)` inside a pcall that keeps the host STOPPED line and re-raises with error(err, 0). The session sweep becomes `S.BulkRun('reset', 'profile', function(info) info.profileReset = true; for _, def in ipairs(Schema) do if def.sessionOnly then S.ApplyDefault(def) end end end)`. Keep BuildDefaults and the popup. (3) settings/Panel.lua:295-296: replace the head splice with `Settings.StampClosureRows(MASTER_ROWS); NS.SchemaRuntime.AddRows(MASTER_ROWS, 1)`. (4) settings/OptionsSetup.lua:194-219: bind the descriptor's get, set, applyDefault, bulkBegin and bulkEnd as values to S members. There is no pre-seam gate. (5) settings/Slash.lua:232-236: bind the descriptor's get, set, findRow and applyDefault to S members. `set = S.Set` means Slash minor 15's CliSet prints the seam's refusal. (6) THE OWNER RULING (route (b), WS-02). locales/enUS.lua adds `L['%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.']`. settings/Slash.lua adds `local function libraryAbsent(verb) NS.Print(L['%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.']:format(verb)) end`. runEnabled (:352-362): `if not H.FindSchema(ENABLED_PATH) then return libraryAbsent(on and '/wg enable' or '/wg disable') end; local ok, err = H.Set(ENABLED_PATH, on); if ok == false then return NS.Print(tostring(err)) end`, then the existing ack. It never acks a write that did not land. runTest (:280-294): for sub '' / 'on' / 'off', `if not H.FindSchema(TEST_MODE_PATH) then return libraryAbsent('/wg test') end` before any Set. 'notify' and the usage lines are unchanged. (7) docs/ARCHITECTURE.md '## Documented deviations' gets a new row. Rule `options-ui-§1`. What differs: on a library-absent load `/wg enable` and `/wg disable` refuse with the library-absent line instead of writing through a Schema writeThrough list, which is the SHOULD route (a). Why: the owner's ruling on WhatGroup#22 (2026-09-23), accepted as a known gap; composers stay hollow and no host copy is written (#73). Decided: <commit date>. Re-check trigger: a report of a library-absent install that needs the switch, options-ui-§1 raising (a) to a MUST, or the owner reopening #22. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md's settings section, docs/schema.md, docs/slash-dispatch.md and docs/settings-panel.md name the seam (NS.SchemaRuntime over LibKa0s-Schema-1.0; settings/SchemaSetup.lua holds the stub). CLAUDE.md: 'nine of LibKa0s's majors' becomes ten (adds Schema), and the seam-file list adds settings/SchemaSetup.lua. Commit body: 'Closes #22'.  Upstream follow-up LK-23 (WhatGroup#22 adoption note) is this item: LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 minor 2 with the writeThrough descriptor field must be in the RV-WG payload, and WhatGroup deliberately passes NO writeThrough list (the owner's route (b) under WS-02). On a library-absent load /wg enable, /wg disable and /wg test on\|off print the WS-02 library-absent line ('/wg enable is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.') and raise no Lua error. The two tests/test_libka0s.lua characterization cases are rewritten to assert that line and the unchanged store, and the options-ui-§1 SHOULD deviation row is recorded in docs/ARCHITECTURE.md. HostSchemaStub copies LK-23's referenceStub (which gains writeThrough) but leaves writeThrough unset.

'degraded: `/wg disable` and `/wg enable` print the library-absent line and write nothing (options-ui-§1, WhatGroup#22)': pcall(OnSlashCommand, 'disable') is ok, the last print ends with '/wg disable is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.', and db.profile.enabled is still true (the unchanged store); the same for 'enable'. 'degraded: `/wg test on\|off` print the library-absent line and move nothing': the line names '/wg test', NS.State.testMode is not true, and the popup was not built. Both are red on the pre-adoption seam, where RawSet writes the row-less path; confirm that, then swap the seam. (b) Run the suite after the swap. The expected reds are exactly the 8 re-pins the Schema doc's 'Adoption notes' sanction, as measured in the 2026-09-23 attempt. RawSet gone: tests/test_settings.lua:310 and tests/test_libka0s.lua:285's member list. opts.skipOnChange/skipRefresh gone: tests/test_settings.lua:318. An unknown path refused (JC-2), two cases. #### WG-23 — ## Message Bus cites the amended architecture-§4 threshold: a shell plus one feature module is below it

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-WG, WG-05, WG-00
- **Findings** WHATGROUP-A-04
- **Change** docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:122-129 '## Message Bus'. Replace the below-threshold reasoning with a citation of architecture-§4 as amended in v2.65.0 (WS-04): the bus MUST applies at two or more feature modules, or at a feature module that registers game events that a second feature module must react to, and the AceAddon object's own handlers are not a feature module. WhatGroup is the shell (core/WhatGroup.lua, which registers the four events) plus one feature module (modules/Frame.lua, which registers none since WG-05). ### ConsumableMaster (30 items)

#### CM-02 — The local AceDB fake strips defaults on a profile switch and raises on bad Copy/Delete, as AceDB-3.0 and kit 26 do

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-CM, LK-03
- **Findings** ConsumableMaster-R-06
- **Change** tests/wow_mock.lua makeAceDB (:386-518). This addon keeps its own AceDB fake instead of the kit's, for two reasons: the kit fires callbacks as plain functions with no CallbackHandler string-method form, and it keeps the store at sv.profiles, not db.profiles. So the LK-03 follow-up ('drop the SetProfile override') becomes this: make the local fake match the kit-26 fidelity, and do not delete it. (a) Add a local removeDefaults(dest, src) that mirrors AceDB-3.0.lua:460-463 over the scalar and plain-sub-table arms. It nils a stored scalar equal to its default, recurses into tables and deletes emptied sub-tables. Add a comment saying the '*'/'**' wildcard arms are unmodeled because defaults/Profile.lua uses neither, and cite libs/AceDB-3.0/AceDB-3.0.lua and tests/_kit/mock_record.lua's kit-26 mirror. (b) db.SetProfile (:491-499) runs removeDefaults(db.profile, defaults.profile) over the OUTGOING profile before it switches. (c) db.CopyProfile raises AceDB's message byte for byte at level 2 in two cases: name == current, and a missing source unless `silent`. Add db.DeleteProfile(name, silent) if absent; it raises on the active profile, and on a missing profile unless silent. Copy the messages from libs/AceDB-3.0/AceDB-3.0.lua:531-537 and :581-587. (d) Rewrite the block comment at :373-384 to say why this fake stays and which kit-26 fidelities it now shares. Grep tests/*.lua for CopyProfile/DeleteProfile calls with a bad name, and correct any that relied on the silent no-op. Change the call, never the assertion.
- **Test first** Write first, in tests/test_profiles.lua, and watch them go red against today's fake. (1) 'AceDB fake: a profile switch strips at-default values from the outgoing profile': set a profile scalar to its default, SetProfile('Other'), and assert db.profiles.Default.<key> == nil. (2) 'the shipped default table survives a switch': SetProfile away and back, then assert #KCM.dbDefaults.profile.macroBar.<a color key> == 4. It stays green here because nothing aliases yet; it is the guard CM-03 turns red. (3) 'CopyProfile onto the active profile raises AceDB's own message', checked with assertErrorMatches on the AceDB text. (4) the same for DeleteProfile of the active profile. Then implement. Add about 4 cases. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the README badge in the same commit.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/ConsumableMaster && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua && lizard -l lua -x "./libs/*" -x "./tests/_kit/*" -C 15 -w . && diff <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list \| tr -d '\r') <(tr -d '\r' < docs/test-cases.md) && wc -l tests/wow_mock.lua (under 1500)

#### CM-05 — Adopt LibKa0s-Options CreateOptionsPanel/OpenOptionsPanel (library parks and self-replays in combat); delete the host park, category-ID capture and expand walk ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-CM, LK-25
- **Findings** ConsumableMaster-R-03, ConsumableMaster-A-04
- **Change** This fixes ConsumableMaster-R-03. LK-25's park replays on a library-private PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED frame whatever the host's stand-down state, so a registration parked while the addon is disabled is no longer lost. (a) settings/OptionsSetup.lua optionsLib:New descriptor: add `buildMain = function(ctx) return Helpers.BuildAboutContent(ctx) end` and `validate = function() Helpers.ValidateSchema() end`. Both are thunks, because Panel.lua loads after this file. Delete the `UI.AceGUI = AceGUI` restatement and its comment (:283-285), because the library now re-resolves AceGUI inside CreateOptionsPanel, and fix the comment at :287-288 that says the addon never calls it. (b) settings/Panel.lua. KCM.Settings.RegisterTab (:1222-1231) keeps recording builders in KCM.Settings.builders. registerPanel (:1233-1297) keeps the libAbsent/sayPanelUnavailable early return and the Settings-API check. It then calls `UI.RegisterOptionsPage(key, key, KCM.Settings.builders[key])` once per key in KCM.Settings.order, guarded by a file-local `pagesQueued`, so the sidebar order is unchanged. Last it calls `UI.CreateOptionsPanel()`. Delete the InCombatLockdown park and its comment (:1253-1270), KCM.Settings.registerPending, KCM.Settings.main/sub and their initializers (:37-39), and `KCM._settingsCategoryID` (:1286). The bootstrap frame (:1302-1311) unregisters both events once registerPanel has handed the request to the library, because the library now owns park and replay. If Helpers.CreatePanel's wrapper adds anything the main canvas needs, fold it into buildMain. (c) core/ConsumableMaster.lua OnRegenEnabled (:651-656): delete the registerPending replay block. CCN drops from 15. (d) settings/OptionsShim.lua: delete expandMainCategory (:184-205). O.Open (:207-236) keeps the libAbsent branch, then `local opened = UI.OpenOptionsPanel()`. true returns true. false means refused in combat, and the library has already printed COMBAT_REFUSED, so return false. nil prints 'settings panel unavailable on this client; use /cm help.' and returns false. Delete sayCombatOpenBlocked if it has no other caller, and its test_locale pin. (e) No Options stub member is added: both call sites sit behind the libAbsent guard, so OPTIONS_SEAM in tests/test_surface_parity.lua is unchanged. (f) docs/ARCHITECTURE.md 'The disabled state is total' -> the 'What SURVIVES, because it is SETUP' row (:197): add 'the settings-category registration, including one parked in combat (LibKa0s-Options-1.0 replays it on its own frame, whatever the stand-down state)'. Also update the :279 Options adoption cell (CreateOptionsPanel/OpenOptionsPanel adopted) and docs/settings-panel.md's registration paragraph.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_settingsui_optionsui.lua. First, a new case, 'a registration parked while the addon is stood down still registers on regen'. It stubs Settings.RegisterCanvasLayoutCategory to return { GetID = function() return 7 end } and counts Settings.RegisterAddOnCategory. The steps: H.SetAndRefresh('enabled', false) out of combat, setCombat(true), KCM.Settings.Register(), setCombat(false), fire PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED to every registered frame (the kit's frame __fire / event dispatch), then assert RegisterAddOnCategory was called once. A second variant stands down IN combat. Both must be red on today's code: the stood-down branch returns before the replay. Falsification comment: 'red under: the replay living only in the host's OnRegenEnabled'. Then rewrite the two existing cases at :769-799 against the library. The first asserts that nothing registers under lockdown and that a second Register while parked is a no-op. (h) docs/midnight-quirks.md: a note on the trade-off, where a rejected name is recorded and skipped instead of raising. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md ## Event Subscriptions: name the helper, KCM.EVENTS and the dump target. docs/slash-dispatch.md: the new dump target.

#### CM-16 — Migration stamps per WS-03: defaults declare schemaVersion 0, the runner owns the stamp and advances it only past a step that returned ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-CM, WS-03
- **Findings** —
- **Change** (a) defaults/Profile.lua:31: `schemaVersion = 0`. Rewrite the comment at :24-30 per savedvariables-§1 (v2.65.0). AceDB's removeDefaults strips a stored value equal to its default, so a default equal to a real version can mask a migration. AceDB backfills a declared default onto a legacy store. 0 has neither problem. (b) core/Database.lua RunMigrations (:163-184): `g.schemaVersion = g.schemaVersion or 0`. migrateProfile (:124-142) keeps `p.schemaVersion or 1`, because a profile with no stamp is a pre-stamp v1 profile and that is the documented one-time cost. Its steps keep the stamp-after-each-step order. Make the invariant explicit: the stamp line sits after each step call, so a raising step leaves the stamp at the last completed version. Add a comment that per-profile steps run on every profile through the OnProfileChanged hook and the per-profile stamp, and are never gated on the account stamp alone. That is WS-03's second allowed shape. Update the file header's two-stamps paragraph. (c) defaults/README.md:17 and docs/profiles.md: schemaVersion 0 and why. (d) core/DebugLogSetup.lua initSummary is unchanged.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_database.lua. (1) 'defaults declare global.schemaVersion = 0' asserts KCM.dbDefaults.global.schemaVersion == 0. Red today, when it is 1. (2) Characterization, green before and after: 'a raising profile step leaves the profile stamp at the last completed version and the account stamp unmoved'. Stub D.MigrateLabelFlagsV3 to error on a v1 profile, pcall D.RunMigrations, then assert p.schemaVersion == 2 and g.schemaVersion unchanged. (3) Characterization: 'every step is idempotent against a fresh default profile'. Run migrateProfile's steps twice on a deep copy of dbDefaults.profile and assert it equals itself.
