#### CM-17 — ConsumableMaster#39: adopt LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 (minor 2) for the settings write seam ⚠

- **Effort** L · **Depends on** RV-CM, CM-03, CM-11, LK-22, LK-17 · **Issues** ConsumableMaster#39
- **Findings** —
- **Change** Built on LK-22 (normalize, SetMany, announceBatch) and LK-17 (CliSet prints the seam's refusal). Read docs/api/Schema/version-2-docs.md first. (1) NEW settings/SchemaStub.lua, loaded in the TOC immediately before settings\OptionsSetup.lua. Add the line only, with no other line moves, and comment it. It is the library-absent stub the Schema doc's 'The degradation stub' prescribes: a trimmed copy of LibKa0s tests/test_schema.lua's referenceStub, write-completing and log-silent, with Get, Set, normalize, SetMany (all-or-nothing, no log), ApplyDefault with the bracket-gated resetExempt, a depth-only BulkBegin/End/Run, and the SplitPath/Read/Write/SameValue primitives. writeThrough is empty (see CM-18). Add a header comment naming the doc section and the deliberate duplication. Publish it as KCM.SchemaStub. (2) settings/Panel.lua. Build `local SchemaLib = LibStub and LibStub("LibKa0s-Schema-1.0", true) or KCM.SchemaStub` and `local S = SchemaLib:New({ rows = KCM.Settings.Schema, resolveRoot = function() return KCM.db and KCM.db.profile, 1 end, announce = announce, announceBatch = announceBatch, debug = function(tag, fmt, ...) if KCM.Debug then KCM.Debug(tag, fmt, ...) end end, debugEnabled = function() return KCM.State and KCM.State.debug end, format = function(_, v) return logValue(v) end, resetExempt = <set built from rows flagged neverReset> })`. Publish it as Helpers.schema. (a) Split VALIDATORS (:885-951) into TYPE_RULES[type] = { validate = fn(value) -> ok, why; normalize = fn(value) -> value \| nil, why }. bool, string and enum are validate-only. number clamps in normalize. color, order, flag-map and plain map copy in normalize. A row that already carries its own `normalize` (stat priority's map) keeps it: that is LK-22's row.normalize signature. H.RegisterRows and the composed-block stamping put validate and normalize on each row by type, closed over the row, and call S.AddRows/S.Reindex. Delete validateSchemaValue, or keep it as a thin read of TYPE_RULES if the CLI coercion uses it. (b) Host reactions move off Schema's onChange. Rows carry the host field `apply` (General, MacroBar, StatPriority and Category decorate maps plus MacroBar.lua's row()/block() helpers at :46-67: `r.apply = r.apply or applyBar`). announce(row, path, value) runs `row.apply` through the existing fireOnChange pcall reporter, then RefreshScalars. announceBatch(writes) runs, through the same reporter, either the batch caller's reactor or each DISTINCT row.apply once in first-seen order (today's runBatchReactors), then RefreshAllPanels if the batch is structural, else RefreshScalars. So a 60-row page reset is still one applyBar and a raising reaction is still reported, not propagated. Before this, grep libs/LibKa0s for any direct call of row.onChange on a path row. If one exists, keep `onChange = apply` on those rows and say so. (c) The diversions become rows. state.debugConsole gets `sessionOnly = true` plus get/set (today's SESSION_PATHS bodies), and global.minimap.hide gets get/set (today's GLOBAL_PATHS bodies, inversion unchanged), both stamped through General.lua's decorate map. Delete SESSION_PATHS, GLOBAL_PATHS, divertedPath, Helpers.Resolve, sameValue, the bulk frame chain (:303-393) and fireOnChange's duplicate. (d) Rebind the seam. Helpers.Get = S.Get. Helpers.Set = S.Set. Helpers.SetAndRefresh(path, v) = `local ok, err, why = S.Set(path, v); if not ok then KCM.Say(('invalid value for %s: %s'):format(path, tostring(why or err))); return false end; return true`. Helpers.SetManyAndRefresh(entries, opts) sets a file-local batchCtx = { onChange = opts.onChange, structural = opts.structural }, calls S.SetMany(entries, { act = opts.bulk and opts.bulk.act, scope = opts.bulk and opts.bulk.scope }), clears batchCtx in every exit, and returns ok. KCM.Schema:Set/SetMany keep their signatures, so the three batch call sites (settings/MacroBar.lua:652, :810, settings/Category.lua:272) do not move. Helpers.FindSchema = S.FindRow. Helpers.Bulk(act, scope, fn) = S.BulkRun(act, scope, function() fn() end). Keep its return only if a caller reads it; grep shows none. Helpers.MuteSetLog(fn) = S.BulkRun('reset', 'profile', function(info) info.profileReset = true; fn() end). Helpers.SilenceOpenBulk: the profile handler (core/ConsumableMaster.lua:473) calls S.ConsumeResetCount() instead, which marks the open bracket as a profile reset, and KCM.ResetAllToDefaults wraps db:ResetProfile in S.ResetCounted so the handler's line carries the count. core/ConsumableMaster.lua:544 restoreSessionRows writes through S.ApplyDefault(row). (3) settings/OptionsSetup.lua descriptor: `get = function(p) return Helpers.Get(p) end` stays, and `set` stays SetAndRefresh because the panel needs the printed refusal. (4) settings/Slash.lua Slash descriptor: set = S.Set directly, so LK-17's CliSet prints INVALID plus why once and the host does not print a second line. findRow = S.FindRow. Point applyDefault (:530-533) at S.ApplyDefault. (5) tests/test_surface_parity.lua: `h.assertSurfaceParity(liveInstance, stubInstance, 'schema instance vs host stub')` and `h.assertSurfaceParity(KCM.SchemaStub, 'LibKa0s-Schema-1.0', {'STRINGS'})`. Register the by-name surface source if the runner uses a table map. (6) Docs. docs/schema.md: rewrite the write-seam section (pipeline, normalize, apply vs onChange, SetMany, the stub). docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:279 Options cell and the adoption table: add the Schema-1.0 row. docs/revendor/2026-09-23-v1.55.0/03_DECISIONS.md is frozen and is not edited. The commit body says 'Closes tusharsaxena/ConsumableMaster#39' and restates the adoption's behavior changes: unknown path refused, tables copied into the store, the log line before the reaction.
- **Test first** Test-first. Characterize before any seam code moves, in a NEW tests/test_schema_adoption.lua, because tests/test_schema.lua is at 1023 and must not grow into the band. Wire it in tests/run.lua SUITES. Pin today's observable behavior through the public seams, where each case passes on the old seam and must still pass on the new one. (a) SetAndRefresh stores a coerced number (a clamp). (b) An enum refusal prints 'invalid value for <path>: allowed values: ...'. (c) An unknown path is refused and stores nothing. (d) A table value is stored as a copy, for color, order and map. (e) A Macro Bar page Defaults reset runs applyBar exactly once and logs exactly one `[Set] reset Macro Bar page: N rows`, with N the changed rows. (f) resetCompositeCategory logs `[Set] reset category KCM_HP_AIO: N rows` and recomputes once. (g) /cm resetall logs only the profile handler's line. (h) The debugConsole row shows and hides the window and is skipped by the global reset. (i) The minimap row inverts and survives both resets. (j) A raising apply is reported by 'onChange for X failed' and the write persists. (k) The degraded build (loadFullAddon(true)) still writes through a host verb that has a row, and prints no [Set] line. Commit these green on the old seam as the first half of the work. The Schema cutover follows, with the suite still green. New red-first cases: (l) row.normalize refusal ('nil, why') reaches the CLI as the library's INVALID line exactly once. It is red before (4). (m) The surface-parity pair in (5). Run tests/perf.lua before and after, since the adoption notes require it for a host with a measured read path, and record both in the commit body. About +14 cases. Regenerate test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/ConsumableMaster && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua && lizard -l lua -x "./libs/*" -x "./tests/_kit/*" -C 15 -w . && diff <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list \| tr -d '\r') <(tr -d '\r' < docs/test-cases.md) && ! git grep -nE 'SESSION_PATHS\|GLOBAL_PATHS\|divertedPath\|Helpers.Resolve\b\|runFrame' -- settings core && wc -l settings/Panel.lua settings/SchemaStub.lua tests/test_schema_adoption.lua (all under 1500; record Panel.lua's new count for CM-30)

#### CM-18 — Degraded (library-absent) composed-row verbs print the library-absent line, never a Lua error or a false success; pin the Slash-stub line with Kit.assertLibraryConstant ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-CM, CM-17, WS-02, LK-18
- **Findings** —
- **Change** Implements WS-02 route (b) and LK-18's stub shape. On a library-absent load, the MasterControls and macro-bar composed rows do not exist (composers answer {}), and there is no Lifecycle latch (core/LifecycleSetup.lua:22 returns early). So a written `enabled` would not be obeyed this session, and route (a) writeThrough would acknowledge a switch nothing honors. (a) locales/enUS.lua: add L["%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load."], a single sentence with one placeholder (slash-commands-§1). (b) settings/Slash.lua: add a local refuseLibraryAbsent(verb), which says that line with '/cm ' .. verb. setEnabled (:109-119) replaces 'settings unavailable.' with refuseLibraryAbsent(on and 'enable' or 'disable') when the row is absent. Rewrite the comment at :93-101 per WS-02. (c) core/SlashCommands.lua. KCM.MacroBar.SetEnabled and SetLocked return the seam's verdict. The BAR_COMMANDS on/off (:873-876), bare `bar` (:904) and runLock print their success line only when the write landed. On a false with a library-absent build, detected through the flag settings/OptionsSetup.lua publishes (KCM.Settings.optionsUI == nil), they print the library-absent line for their verb. Otherwise they print the seam's refusal. So no false 'macro bar ON'. (d) docs/ARCHITECTURE.md Documented deviations: a row keyed `options-ui-§1` (the WS-02 composed-row ruling). What differs: /cm enable and /cm disable take route (b), refusing with the library-absent line, instead of writeThrough. Why: a library-absent build has no stand-down latch, so a stored switch would not take effect this session. Decided <date>. Re-check trigger: a library-absent latch stub, or a runtime reader of `enabled` outside the latch. (e) LK-18 pin. `git grep DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT -- core settings` proves that no host copy of the disabled refusal exists: the degraded arm has no latch, so it has no disabled state to refuse. State that in a test comment. Pin the one host copy that does exist, the locale string for the library-absent line, with `h.assertLibraryConstant(L[...], "LibKa0s-Slash-1.0", <member path the Slash version-15 doc's '## The degradation stub' section names for the line>)`. If the doc publishes no such member, assert the string byte for byte against WS-02's text and note it in the commit body. Also pin the live refusal: `h.assertLibraryConstant` on the format the live Sl:DisabledLine() is built from, 'DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT'.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_slash.lua's degraded block (loadFullAddon(true)). For each of 'enable', 'disable', 'bar', 'bar on', 'bar off', 'lock', 'unlock': run `local ok, err = pcall(KCM.OnSlashCommand, KCM, verb)`, then assert ok (no Lua error), exactly one printed line equal to the library-absent line for that verb, and an unchanged store. Red before: enable prints 'settings unavailable.' and 'bar on' prints 'macro bar ON' over a refused write. Falsification comment: 'red under: printing the success line without checking SetEnabled's verdict'. Add the assertLibraryConstant case(s) from (e). About +8 cases. Regenerate test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** cd /mnt/d/Profile/Users/Tushar/Documents/GIT/ConsumableMaster && luacheck . && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua && ~/.claude/wow-addon/bin/ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua && lizard -l lua -x "./libs/*" -x "./tests/_kit/*" -C 15 -w . && diff <(lua5.1 tests/run.lua --list \| tr -d '\r') <(tr -d '\r' < docs/test-cases.md) && ! git grep -n 'settings unavailable' -- settings core
- **Smoke** In client, on a test install with libs/LibKa0s renamed away: /cm enable, /cm bar on and /cm lock each print one '... is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.' line, and no Lua error appears with BugSack enabled.

(2) CM.Sync non-deferred branch (:107-108): after `inst:Destroy()`, set `dormant[id] = inst`. destroyParked (:114-119): after `inst:Destroy()`, move the instance to `dormant[id]` instead of dropping it. (3) follow() (:85-93): add a branch after `retiring[id]`: `elseif dormant[id] then local d = dormant[id]; dormant[id] = nil; d.staleData = true; revive(id, d, hold)`. Only when no instance exists anywhere does it call NS.Container.New(id). staleData=true is required because the id may name a different container now, and CM.Announce's RequestApply rebuilds it for the new data and unparks it. (4) If the round-trip test shows the anchor still hidden or unpointed after that apply (Destroy runs anchor:Hide() and ClearAllPoints, modules/Container.lua:539-546), add `ContainerClass:Revive()` to modules/Container.lua, called from revive() for a dormant instance, which clears `retired` state as Apply requires. Otherwise add nothing. (5) Add the test seam `function CM.__dormant() return dormant end`. (6) Rewrite the :51-63 comment: a destroyed instance is kept dormant under its id and revived when the id returns, so CreateFrame('Frame','AuraMasterAnchor'..id) runs once per id per session.

#### AM-05 — Degraded latch is edge-triggered like the library: SyncEnabled stands down or up only on a change

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-04
- **Findings** AuraMaster-R-09
- **Change** core/LifecycleSetup.lua degraded branch (:120-131). Add `local down = false`, the library's empty-latch state, so the first SyncEnabled on an enabled load is a no-op as it is with LibKa0s-Lifecycle. `function NS.SyncEnabled() local want = not enabledStored(); if want == down then return end; down = want; if want then standDown() else standUp() end end`. `NS.IsStoodDown` and `NS.IsDisabled` read `down` rather than re-reading the store, so the show ladder and the edge agree. Comment: this mirrors the library's edge semantics, which is a correctness property of the degraded path and not a library-stack-§7 member copy.
- **Test first** Test first in tests/test_lifecycle.lua (it can load tests/degraded_env.lua; degraded_env is not a suite). 'lifecycle: the degraded latch stands up and down only on an edge': load degraded, count the timers a RequestApply arms (#mocks.__timers) and the lifecycle registrations (mocks.__registrations filtered to NS.addon). Call NS2.SyncEnabled() twice with enabled unchanged and assert no new timer and no second registration of PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD. Mark it `-- red under: the level-triggered stub`. Then set profile.enabled=false and SyncEnabled: registrations gone. Then true: back once. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** luacheck . -> 0/0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua -> exit 0 (tests/test_setups.lua:115 'degraded: ... every seam answers' still green); ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua -> exit 0; lizard ... -C 15 -w . -> no new warning; the 1500-line check -> only the #16-#19 files.

#### AM-07 — Every event registration goes through LibKa0s-Core's SafeRegisterEvent; rejected names are kept in NS.RejectedEvents and shown in [Init] ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-06, LK-11
- **Findings** AuraMaster-R-05
- **Change** Consumer follow-up for LK-11 (events-frames-taint-§1, WS-04). (1) core/CoreSetup.lua. Above the `if not lib` branch, add `NS.RejectedEvents = NS.RejectedEvents or {}` so the parity derivation sees it in both halves. Live half: `NS.SafeRegisterEvent = lib.SafeRegisterEvent; NS.SafeRegisterUnitEvent = lib.SafeRegisterUnitEvent; NS.SafeRegisterEvents = lib.SafeRegisterEvents`. Degraded half: one-rung bodies with the library's signatures, pcall and no IsEventValid front gate, as the Core version-8 doc's Degradation note prescribes. SafeRegisterEvent(target, event, handler, rejected) runs `pcall(target.RegisterEvent, target, event, handler)`, appends the name to `rejected` once on failure, and returns the ok boolean. SafeRegisterUnitEvent(frame, event, rejected, u1, u2) does the same through RegisterUnitEvent. SafeRegisterEvents walks an array and returns the count registered. (2) core/AuraMaster.lua. Add a module-level `local LIFECYCLE_EVENTS = { {'PLAYER_ENTERING_WORLD','OnEnterWorld'}, {'PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED','OnCombatChanged'}, {'PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED','OnCombatChanged'}, {'PLAYER_TARGET_CHANGED','OnUnitSwap'}, {'PLAYER_FOCUS_CHANGED','OnUnitSwap'}, {'UNIT_PET','OnUnitPet'}, {'ADDON_LOADED','OnAddonLoaded'}, {'ADDON_RESTRICTION_STATE_CHANGED','OnRestrictionChanged'} }`. RegisterLifecycleEvents loops it through NS.SafeRegisterEvent(self, e[1], e[2], NS.RejectedEvents), and UnregisterLifecycleEvents loops the same list, replacing the hand-kept copy at :69-77. (3) modules/TimedSpells.lua: TS.Sync (:140-142) and syncAuraListen (:121) register through NS.SafeRegisterEvent(events, ...). `listening` is set only when the registration returned true. (4) core/LifecycleSetup.lua holdPending (:71): `pendingHeld = NS.SafeRegisterEvent(addon, PENDING_EVENT, fn, NS.RejectedEvents)`. On false the hold is not taken and the secure half is retried on the next stand-down or up. (5) core/DebugLogSetup.lua initSummary (:91-99): append `, rejected events: A, B` when #NS.RejectedEvents > 0. It is read at call time, and the library calls it when logging turns on (libs/LibKa0s/DebugLog.lua:766). A rejection after that point is also traced with NS.Debug('Init', 'event %s rejected by this client', name) at the moment it happens. (6) docs: the ARCHITECTURE.md '## Event Subscriptions' section says every registration goes through the helper and where rejects surface; docs/midnight-quirks.md records the probe-vs-lost-edge trade (events-frames-taint-§1 last bullet).
- **Test first** Test first. tests/test_lifecycle.lua 'lifecycle: one bad event name leaves the other seven registered and is recorded': a fresh env with mocks.__badEvents = { ADDON_RESTRICTION_STATE_CHANGED = true } set before OnEnable. Assert that the other seven names are in mocks.__registrations() for NS.addon, that NS.RejectedEvents contains the name exactly once after a disable/enable cycle, and that enabling logging prints an [Init] line naming it. Mark it `-- red under: bare self:RegisterEvent`. With kit 26, set C_EventUtils = nil in a second case so only the pcall rung runs. tests/test_timedspells.lua: the same with UNIT_AURA bad, where TimedSpells is not marked listening and the rest of the addon loads. tests/test_surface_parity.lua 'parity: the Core stub ...' stays green, because it derives the three new publications from core/CoreSetup.lua. A degraded-env case: the stub's SafeRegisterEvent records a bad name. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** luacheck . -> 0/0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua -> exit 0; grep -nE ':RegisterEvent\(\|:RegisterUnitEvent\(' core modules settings \| grep -v SafeRegister -> no call outside core/CoreSetup.lua's stub bodies; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua -> exit 0; lizard ... -C 15 -w . -> no new warning, RegisterLifecycleEvents CCN <= 3; the 1500-line check -> only the #16-#19 files.
- **Smoke** In client: /am debug on shows the [Init] line with no 'rejected events' clause on 12.1; /am disable then /am enable, then /dump AuraMaster... NS.RejectedEvents via /run print(#select(2,...)) is not reachable in chat, so use /am debug on after a /reload and read the [Init] line.

#### AM-10 — Migration stamp per WS-03: defaults declare schemaVersion 0, the stamp advances only past a clean step, and every stored profile is migrated ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-09, WS-03
- **Findings** —
- **Change** WS-03 consumer follow-up. defaults/Profile.lua:84-88: `schemaVersion = 0`. Rewrite the comment: AceDB strips a stored value equal to its default at logout, and backfills a declared default onto a legacy account with no stamp, and 0 is safe against both (savedvariables-§1 v2.65.0). core/Database.lua NS.RunMigrations (:897-908): `g.schemaVersion = g.schemaVersion or 0`. Each step runs as `local ok, err = pcall(step.apply, NS.db)`. On failure it stops the ladder with the stamp unchanged, prints ONE line through NS.Printf(L['%s: migration to schema v%s failed; your settings were left as they were. %s'], ...), and returns without stamping. The rest of InitDB still runs, so the addon still loads. On success it stamps step.to. Database.CurrentSchemaVersion's fallback (:892-895) becomes `or 0`. Declare `NS.SCHEMA_VERSION = Database.CurrentSchemaVersion()` as the runner's target. Every step already walks eachProfile over the raw db.sv.profiles (:812-827). Add a sentence to SCHEMA_STEPS' header that this is the per-profile rule and that every step is idempotent against a fresh default profile, which a fresh install (stamp 0) runs. Add the new L key to locales/enUS.lua. docs/schema.md migration section: stamp ownership, 0, pcall, per-profile.
- **Test first** Test first in tests/test_database.lua. (a) 'migrations: a legacy v1 account with NO stamp runs every step': build a raw SV with a v1-shaped profile and no global.schemaVersion. Before the fix AceDB backfills the default 1, which happens to be right, so pair it with (b). (b) 'migrations: a stored stamp equal to the default is not lost at logout': with the kit-26 fake stripping defaults on SetProfile/logout, store schemaVersion at the default and reload. Assert that the reloaded stamp still reads as the current version, or that the steps re-run idempotently with no change to a migrated profile. Mark it `-- red under: default = 1 with a real v1 store` and state the exact failing branch in the comment. (c) 'migrations: a step that raises leaves the stamp where it was and the addon loads': monkeypatch one step to error, and assert the stamp is at the previous version, one failure line was printed, and NS.db exists. Mark it `-- red under: stamping without pcall`. (d) 'migrations: an inactive profile is migrated too': two stored v5 profiles, and both get the v6 stamping. Also assert 'every step is idempotent on a fresh default profile': run the ladder twice over a fresh profile and assert it is deep-equal. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** luacheck . -> 0/0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua -> exit 0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua -> exit 0; grep -n 'schemaVersion' defaults/Profile.lua -> '= 0'; lizard ... -C 15 -w . -> no new warning, NS.RunMigrations CCN <= 6; the 1500-line check -> only the #16-#19 files.
- **Smoke** In client, with a BACKUP of WTF/Account/<acct>/SavedVariables/AuraMasterDB.lua: hand-edit a copy to delete global.schemaVersion; log in with /am debug on: the [Migrate] lines run v2..v6 (or no-op idempotently), /reload shows the stamp persisted ('schema v6' in [Init]); restore the backup.

Mark it `-- red under: the host wrapper printing and returning nil`. 'slash verbs: /am reset container.name prints the library's no-default line once': assert the line equals SlashLib.STRINGS.NO_DEFAULT:format('container.name') and that no echo follows. Re-point any existing case that expected the host's printed reason. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.

#### AM-15 — AuraMaster#21: adopt LibKa0s-Schema-1.0's primitives, registry, bulk bracket, SameValue and Validate, with the host arm kept for the library-less build ⚠

- **Effort** L · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-14, LK-22 · **Issues** AuraMaster#21
- **Findings** —
- **Change** The LK-22 consumer follow-up, taking the adoption mapping from the v1.56.0 docs/api/Schema/version-2-docs.md AuraMaster note and Ka0sAddonsCommonTasks/docs/2026-09-22-SUITE_STANDARDS_AND_LIBKA0S_SWEEP/3b-specs/schema.md:520-538. settings/Schema.lua. Add `local SchemaLib = LibStub and LibStub('LibKa0s-Schema-1.0', true)`. (1) Primitives: `splitPath`/`readFrom`/`writeInto` (:88-120) become thin locals calling lib.SplitPath / lib.Read(root, parts, first) / lib.Write(root, parts, value, first) when SchemaLib is present, else the existing host bodies. The host bodies stay as the library-absent arm and are renamed hostSplitPath etc. (2) Registry: `local S = SchemaLib and SchemaLib:New{ rows = NS.Schema }`, passing neither resolveRoot nor announce. FindSchemaRow uses S.FindRow when S exists. RegisterSchemaRows keeps its default stamping, then calls S.AddRows(rows, indexOf(beforePath)). UnregisterSchemaRows keeps its in-place rebuild and ends with S.Reindex(). (3) Bracket: NS.Bulk = { Begin = S.BulkBegin, End = S.BulkEnd, Run = S.BulkRun } when S exists. tally(n) calls S.BulkAdd(n), and every `bulk.depth > 0` test (:456, :485, :501, :617, :648, :686) calls an `inBulk()` that maps to S.InBulk(). Bulk.Run callers that returned true now set `info.profileReset = true` (JC-9): the degraded RestoreAllDefaults in settings/OptionsSetup.lua:295-306. modules/ContainerManager.lua copyThrough and ResetPositions return nothing and are unchanged. (4) changes() (:427-431) is `not lib.SameValue(old, new)` when present, which drops the FilterCompiler.Signature dependency on the live path. The spell-id sets are integer-keyed after normalizeIdSet. (5) NS.ValidateSchema (:819-835) runs S.Validate{ pages = VALID_PAGES, defaultsRoot = function(parts) if parts[1]=='container' then return NS.CONTAINER_TEMPLATE, 2 elseif parts[1]=='global' then return NS.defaults, 1 end return NS.defaults.profile, 1 end } and returns errors + missing. The host loop stays as the absent arm. (6) RE-EVALUATION OF Set (#21's triggers fired: row.normalize exists and ConsumableMaster is its second consumer). DECISION: keep NS.SetByPath and do not adopt S.Set. The front branches have no row-shaped equivalent in S.Set: the minimap inversion, the CARVE_OUTS whole-set normalizers, the whole-SECTION all-or-nothing writes, and NS.CheckWrite's dry run that must mirror them. The library-less build must keep the host seam in any case, so adopting Set would give the live and degraded builds two different write paths for the same rows, which is the cost the spec's verify round measured. Record this in docs/schema.md ('Write seam: why AuraMaster keeps SetByPath') with a re-check trigger: 'LibKa0s-Schema gains a resolve hook for container-relative paths plus section writes, or the standard makes Set adoption a requirement'. Update the header comment of settings/Schema.lua. After the owner merges, comment on #21 with the decision and label it state:done, spacing each gh write at least 5 s apart.
- **Test first** Characterization first: the existing suites pin the seam (tests/test_schema.lua, test_schema_paths.lua, test_bulklog.lua incl. :99 'the degraded build's Reset all is one line in total', test_setups.lua:115, test_slash_verbs.lua:616). They must stay green unchanged, apart from JC-9 re-pins. New cases: tests/test_schema.lua 'schema: with LibKa0s the bracket, registry and validator are the library's'. Assert NS.Bulk.Begin == SchemaLib instance BulkBegin, NS.FindSchemaRow(path) == S.FindRow(path) for every row, and NS.ValidateSchema() == 0. Mark it `-- red under: the host copies on the live path`. 'schema: without LibKa0s the host arm still answers' is a degraded-env case asserting SetByPath, the bracket's one line and ValidateSchema == 0. #### AM-16 — Degraded Options stub composers answer {}; enable/disable/lock write through a declared writeThrough list; the Slash stub's lines are the library's own ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-15, LK-18, LK-23, WS-02
- **Findings** AuraMaster-R-06
- **Change** WS-02 / LK-23 / LK-18 consumer follow-ups. (1) settings/OptionsSetup.lua stub (:175-280). Delete composeBlock. ColorPair, FontGroup, BorderGroup and BarGroup answer `{}`. MasterControls answers `{}, function() end`. Keep MASTER_GROUP and RestoreAllDefaults. Rewrite the header (:162-173): per options-ui-§1 (v2.64.0+), a library-absent stub's composers answer an empty row list, and a host copy of a composed block is anti-pattern #73. (2) Route (a) for host verbs on composed rows. settings/Schema.lua declares `NS.WRITE_THROUGH = { enabled = true, locked = true }`, data only. In NS.SetByPath, a path with no indexed row that IS in NS.WRITE_THROUGH is stored raw into db.profile with writeInto(copy(value)), with no validate, normalize or onChange, is tallied, and is announced with announceWrite(nil, nil, path, value, false, false), returning true. A path with a row always takes the row. This mirrors LK-23's library semantics for AuraMaster's own seam, which AM-15 kept. When SchemaLib exists, the same list is also handed to S.New{ writeThrough = {'enabled','locked'} } so a future Set adoption inherits it. settings/Slash.lua runEnabled calls NS.SyncEnabled() after a successful write, which is idempotent on the live path where the row's onChange already ran and required on the degraded path where no row exists. runTest drives NS.Preview directly and needs no route. (3) settings/Slash.lua stub. absent(verb) prints the WS-02 library-absent line through the locale as one sentence, `printf(L['%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.'], '/am ' .. verb)`, replacing L['/am %s is unavailable. %s.'], which is deleted from locales/enUS.lua. stub.DisabledLine (:388-391) uses the library's exact bytes: a file-level `local STUB_DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT = '%s is disabled \226\128\148 enable it with \|cFFFFFF00%s\|r'` formatted with (brandName or slash, slash .. ' enable'), and published for the test as `Sl.__stubDisabledLineFormat` in both branches. (4) NS.ValidateSchema and every degraded consumer tolerate the smaller schema. CM's CONFIG_CHANGED handler already treats a nil row as the default apply. (5) docs/settings-panel.md 'The degraded panel': the composed rows are absent, /am set on them prints the library-absent line, and enable/disable/lock/unlock still work through writeThrough. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md '## The disabled state' names the route.
- **Test first** Test first. tests/test_optionssetup.lua:369: replace `#NS2.Schema == #NS.Schema` with three pins: the full-load count equals N_full, the library-absent count equals N_degraded, and the delta equals the named composer rows. Compute the delta from the live composers' outputs so it is derived, not typed, and mark it `-- red under: a non-empty stub composer`. tests/test_options_descriptor.lua:445 'the stub's composers emit the paths and types the live composers do' is replaced by 'every stub composer answers an empty row list'. Fall-together case (tests/test_slash_verbs.lua): on a degraded load, `/am set container.bars.fontSize 12` and `/am set enabled false` each print exactly '/am set is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.' and write nothing. tests/test_slash.lua:233: re-point it off recordSeam so the degraded `/am disable` / `/am enable` really store db.profile.enabled false/true through writeThrough with no row, and the latch follows (NS2.IsStoodDown()). Mark it `-- red under: writeThrough absent (Setting not found)`. The degraded `/am lock` does the same for locked. tests/test_surface_parity.lua: `T.assertLibraryConstant(NS2.Slash.__stubDisabledLineFormat, 'LibKa0s-Slash-1.0', 'DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT')`, marked `-- red under: the library rewording`. tests/test_slash_verbs.lua:581 re-points to the new absent line. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** luacheck . -> 0/0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua -> exit 0; grep -n 'composeBlock' settings/OptionsSetup.lua -> nothing; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua -> exit 0; lizard ... -C 15 -w . -> none; the 1500-line check -> only the #16-#19 files.
- **Smoke** In client, on a throwaway copy of the addon with libs/LibKa0s renamed: /reload; /am disable and /am enable toggle the containers with no Lua error; /am set enabled false prints the library-absent line; /am lock locks; restore the folder.

#### AM-17 — Adopt O.RenderTabbedSchema's tabs/disabledFor/disabledNotice/chrome and O.PageBanner's action; delete the host tab renderer and header fork ⚠

- **Effort** L · **Depends on** RV-AM, AM-16, LK-27, LK-28
- **Findings** AuraMaster-R-04
- **Change** LK-28 consumer follow-up (C-04b; library-stack-§5, anti-pattern #47). settings/OptionsSetup.lua. Delete collectTabs (:560-580), settleActiveTab (:583-588), renderActiveTab (:624-640), drawDisabledNotice (:604-608), renderBespoke (:613-619), placeTab (:548-557), Helpers.RenderTabbedPage (:659-685), buildContainerHeader (:480-504), placeInHeader and the HEADER_* constants (:458-478), and Helpers.ContainerHeader (:521-535). Replace them with one host wrapper, `Helpers.RenderPage(ctx, pageKey, spec, banner)`, that maps AuraMaster's page spec onto the library opts. `tabs`: spec.tabs filtered by `auraTypes` against the active container at render time, each wrapped as `render = function(c) t.render(c, cfg, rowsOfGroup(t.key)) end`. A tab keyed by a schema group replaces that group's rows, as the v1.56.0 Options doc specifies. If that doc says otherwise, STOP and raise it upstream; never fork. `disabledFor` and `disabledNotice` pass through. The muted-red NOTICE_COLOR stays via disabledNotice returning the colored string. `chrome = function(c) if spec.intro and cfg then spec.intro(c, cfg) end end` (the chrome hook runs after the strip and before rows). The empty-registry page becomes a single `tabs` entry `{ key = '__empty', label = L['Container'], render = <the 'No containers yet' TextRow> }` when there is no cfg and the page is not addonWide. Banners: Helpers.ContainerBanner keeps O.PageBanner, and the Containers page's banner becomes `Helpers.PageBanner(ctx, { label, tooltip, list, order, value, onSelect, action = { text = L['New container'], tooltip = ..., onClick = spec.onNew } })` (options-ui-§14 picker+create band). Helpers.RenderContainerPage (:688-690) calls Helpers.RenderPage with ContainerBanner. General (settings/General.lua:186) and Containers (settings/Containers.lua:244-269) call Helpers.RenderPage. Delete Containers.lua's header() and its __chromeWidgets release wrapper (:244-269), because the library owns widget release. LK-27: remove every host write of `ctx.__bannerWidget` (ContainerBanner :424, :495, :525, :666), since the library keeps and releases it. Update the stub member list (:311-316): drop 'ContainerHeader' and 'RenderTabbedPage' and add 'RenderPage'. The header comment names the library as the renderer.
- **Test first** Characterization first, on the old code: for each of the seven pages (general, containers, filters, layout, bars, icons, text), pin the drawn tab keys and labels in order, the active-tab heal after a container switch that removes the active tab, the disabled notice text and disabled state on Bars/Icons/Text for a mismatched style, the Filters intro warnings above rows, the empty-registry line, and the Containers band holding a picker and a New container button. Use tests/page_helpers.lua and the TabStrip / PageBanner widgets the kit records. Commit those pins green on the old code. Then swap and keep them green. Re-point the test_optionssetup and test_pages_* cases that read ctx.__tabs or ctx.__chromeWidgets to the library's documented seam or the recorded TabStrip. New leak case using kit 26's M.__aceguiLive('Dropdown'): rendering the Filters page 10 times and the Containers page 10 times leaves the live Dropdown and Button counts flat. Mark it `-- red under: the host clearing ctx.__bannerWidget`. Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.
- **Verify** grep -nE 'collectTabs\|settleActiveTab\|renderActiveTab\|RenderTabbedPage\|buildContainerHeader\|__bannerWidget\s*=' settings/ -> nothing; luacheck . -> 0/0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/run.lua -> exit 0; ka0s-bounded lua5.1 tests/perf.lua -> exit 0; lizard ... -C 15 -w . -> none; wc -l settings/OptionsSetup.lua shrinks; the 1500-line check -> only the #16-#19 files.
- **Smoke** In client: open every page; each draws its tab strip; switch tabs; select a container whose aura type hides Categories and Overrides, and the strip heals to the first tab; Bars on an icons container shows the muted-red notice and disabled controls; Containers shows the picker and New container on one band and New container creates one; flip containers 20 times and /dump collectgarbage('count') stays flat.

#### AM-19 — One enabled predicate, and the dead pre-11.0 GetMouseFocus rung deleted ⚠

- **Findings** AuraMaster-R-13

Grep core/Compat.lua for any other `_G.<Global>` rung that 120100 does not provide and delete it too, listing each in the commit body. With _G.GetMouseFoci = nil and _G.GetMouseFocus stubbed to return a frame, Compat.GetMouseFocus() answers nil. Mark it `-- red under: the _G.GetMouseFocus fallback`. tests/test_slash.lua: 'slash: the dispatcher's isEnabled is NS.EnabledStored' asserts `NS.Slash.__cli` was built with the same function (a descriptor spy) or behaviorally matches before InitDB (nil read -> enabled). Regenerate docs/test-cases.md and the badge.

#### KC-02 — tests/run.lua stops swallowing an OnInitialize raise

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-KC
- **Findings** KICKCD-R-11
- **Change** tests/run.lua:105 `if initDB and NS.OnInitialize then pcall(NS.OnInitialize, NS) end` becomes `local ok, err = pcall(...)`, and the error is kept on the instance (inst.initError). loadInstance raises `error("OnInitialize raised: " .. tostring(err), 0)` unless opts.allowInitError is set. The shared instance (:115 `loadInstance(true)`) therefore fails the whole run loudly. Rewrite the :106-108 comment, which currently calls the swallow deliberate. Before committing, run the suite under the strict version and list every case that depended on the swallow (expected: degraded-environment loads with libFiles = {}). Each such case passes { allowInitError = true } and asserts the error it expects, or the underlying init raise is fixed if it is a real defect. Record the list in the commit body. The other KICKCD-R-11 gaps land with their fixes: SetProfile in KC-03, the frame delta in KC-05, the throttle count in KC-10, the render count in KC-11 and the released-widget state in KC-12.
- **Test first** Red first: add a case to tests/test_database.lua, 'a raising OnInitialize fails T.load instead of passing silently', with the line '-- red under: tests/run.lua pcall-swallowing OnInitialize'. It uses T.load(true, false, function(m) m.__libs['AceDB-3.0'].New = function() error('boom') end end) inside assertError, and a second call with { allowInitError = true } asserts inst.initError contains 'boom'. On HEAD the first call returns normally, so the case is red.

#### KC-03 — Migrations: schemaVersion 0 in defaults, the stamp advances only past a clean step, and the color and font-flag steps run for every profile ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-02
- **Findings** KICKCD-R-01, KICKCD-A-08
- **Change** core/Database.lua. (1) WS-03: aceDBDefaults() (:70-72) declares `global.schemaVersion = 0`. Rewrite the comment: AceDB's removeDefaults strips a stamp equal to its default, and a current-version default also masks a legacy account, while 0 has neither problem. (2) The migrations table (:713-721) holds pure steps with no stamp writes: [1] FoldLegacyUnits, [2] MigrateSpecKeys, [3] MigrateColorShape, [4] MigrateFontFlags. (3) MigrateProfile (:734-767): keep the legacy profile.dbVersion adoption. `local v = g.schemaVersion or 0; if v < 1 then v = 1 end`, because 0 is a fresh or stripped-stamp account and every step is idempotent against a default profile. Loop: `local ok, err = pcall(step, self.db)`. On ok, `g.schemaVersion = v + 1`. On failure, log one NS.Util.print line 'settings migration v%d -> v%d failed: %s' plus NS.Debug('Migrate', ...), then break without advancing. Keep the missing-step jump. (4) KICKCD-R-01: Database:Init (:866-876) and OnProfileChanged (:821-824) call `self:MigrateColorShape(self.db)` and `self:MigrateFontFlags(self.db)` right after MigrateSpecKeys, so every profile is converted the first time it is active. MigrateColorShape gains `if not (db and db.profile) then return end`. Its walk becomes walk(t, d, depth), with d the NS.DEFAULT_PROFILE subtree at the same key. For a hybrid color, if the keyed part SameValue-equals d's keyed part (or d is absent), convert array -> keys as today. Otherwise the keys are a post-upgrade edit, so keep them and nil out [1]..[4]. State the rule in the docstring and in docs/schema.md's migration section. (5) KICKCD-A-08: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:390 savedvariables-§1 row 1 is rewritten, not retired. What differs names all five shape-driven steps (FoldLegacyUnits, BackfillLabelStyle, MigrateSpecKeys, MigrateColorShape, MigrateFontFlags) run on Init and every OnProfileChanged beside the version ladder. The trigger is restated as 'a sixth shape-driven step, or a LibKa0s per-profile migration runner'. Replace the schema.md#L256 anchor with the heading anchor for '### `units.<unit>.label.style` shape' (reasoning at schema.md:271). Row 2's schema.md#L266 becomes the '## Migration: folding legacy `icons`/`castbar`/`anchors` into `units.target`' anchor (reasoning at :281). ARCHITECTURE.md:468 load-order prose lists five steps. (6) tests/wow_mock.lua AceDB fake (:454-632): a real SetProfile(name). `db.__profiles` is seeded from the staged SV `profiles` table, and each profile is copyDefaults-merged on first access with its identity kept per name. SetProfile sets db.keys.profile, swaps db.profile and fires ('OnProfileChanged', db, name) through the existing fire(). GetCurrentProfile answers db.keys.profile, and GetProfiles lists the keys.

#### KC-04 — Adopt Core.SafeRegisterEvent: every event block survives one bad name, with a rejected list, a debug verb and an [Init] note ⚠

(5) tests/wow_mock.lua: mocks.CreateFrame sets f.__mocks = mocks. FRAME_METHODS.RegisterEvent and RegisterUnitEvent (:385-394) raise `Attempt to register unknown event "<NAME>"`, the kit mock_base message, when the name is in self.__mocks.__badEvents, read at call time. #### KC-05 — One cast filter frame per (module, unit), armed and disarmed rather than rebuilt, carrying the EMPOWER events ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-04
- **Findings** KICKCD-R-03, KICKCD-R-06
- **Change** (1) core/Util.lua:419-445: replace Util.RegisterUnitCastEvent with `Util.NewUnitCastFilter(module, unit, routes)`. It errors if any routes key does not start with 'UNIT_SPELLCAST_' (the helper's one job; events-frames-taint-§1 forbids a general frame factory). It creates ONE frame whose OnEvent calls `module[routes[event]](module, event, evUnit, ...)` when that handler exists. It returns { frame = f, armed = false, Arm = fn, Disarm = fn }. Arm is idempotent: when not armed, it registers exactly the routes' fixed event set through NS.SafeRegisterUnitEvent(f, ev, NS.State.rejectedEvents, unit) and sets armed. Disarm calls f:UnregisterAllEvents() and clears armed. Nothing is allocated per arm. (2) modules/IconGrid.lua: a file-scope ICON_CAST_ROUTES maps the 8 existing events plus UNIT_SPELLCAST_EMPOWER_START, _UPDATE and _STOP to 'OnUnitCastEvent'. #### KC-06 — core/SpellInput.lua: one resolver, the Cooldown Manager gate on the CLI, multi-word names, validated class and spec, and an AceEvent cache invalidator ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-04
- **Findings** KICKCD-R-05, KICKCD-R-18, KICKCD-A-04
- **Change** (1) New core/SpellInput.lua (NS.SpellInput). Its TOC line goes right after core\KickCD.lua with '# LOAD-BEARING POSITION: calls NS.NewBusTarget (core/KickCD.lua) at file load; everything else it reads resolves at call time.' It contains: SpellInput.Resolve(input), the single copy of core/KickCD.lua:570-584 resolveSpellInput and settings/Spells.lua:272-285 validateSpellInput. The Cooldown Manager cache moves from settings/Spells.lua:287-384 (cooldownViewerApi, collectCategorySpells, getCooldownManagerSpellSet, _cmCache/_CM_EMPTY, invalidate). SpellInput.IsLivePair(class, spec). SpellInput.Admissible(id, class, spec) returns true, or false plus the reason line 'Spell %s (#%d) is not tracked by the Blizzard Cooldown Manager for this specialization.', applied only when (class, spec) is the player's live pair, as the page does today. SpellInput.ParseTail(tokens), a greedy longest-prefix name match: 'Wind Shear' resolves before 'Wind'. Trailing tokens become CLASS and SPEC only if CLASS is a known class file token (walk GetClassInfo 1..GetNumClasses) and SPEC resolves for that class through Util.ResolveSpecID; otherwise it returns nil with 'Unknown class <x>' or 'Unknown spec <y> for <CLASS>'. The invalidator is an eager file-scope `local ev = NS.NewBusTarget()`; SpellInput.Arm() uses NS.RegisterEventList(ev, { {'TRAIT_CONFIG_UPDATED', invalidate}, {'PLAYER_SPECIALIZATION_CHANGED', invalidate} }) and runs at file load. SpellInput.StandDown() is ev:UnregisterAllEvents(), and SpellInput.StandUp() is `_cmCache = nil; Arm()`. core/LifecycleSetup.lua standDown and standUp call both beside SpellsPanel. This removes the settings/Spells.lua cacheEvents CreateFrame, the KICKCD-A-04 Spells half. (2) settings/Spells.lua: delete the moved code and call NS.SpellInput.*. Spells.StandDown and StandUp stop touching cacheEvents, and Spells.ValidateSpellInput stays as an alias of SpellInput.Resolve for the harness. (3) core/KickCD.lua: spellsAdd (:617-635) uses SpellInput.ParseTail and SpellInput.Admissible; resolveSpellInput is deleted; the bare `/kcd spells` line (:764) prints sd(spc). (4) core/Database.lua AddSpell (:350): refuse a class token that is neither a key of NS.DefaultSpells nor a GetClassInfo file token, answering nil, 'unknown class', without calling EnsureSpellList. (5) docs/ARCHITECTURE.md module-map row and docs/module-map.md row for core/SpellInput.lua.

(c) '`spells add <id> WARLORD 99999` writes nothing' asserts profile.spells.WARLORD == nil; it is red on HEAD. (d) 'bare `/kcd spells` names the default spec by SpecDisplay' is red on HEAD. tests/test_spell_registry.lua: Resolve by id, by name and unknown; ParseTail multi-word plus class and spec; 'the CM cache is invalidated by TRAIT_CONFIG_UPDATED even when the Spells page was never built' (fire through mocks.__fireEvent). tests/test_disabled.lua keeps asserting an empty registration set while disabled, now including SpellInput's target.

#### KC-07 — core/State.lua's combat listener becomes an AceEvent target, and the dead PLAYER_LOGIN re-registration goes ⚠

- **Effort** S · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-04, KC-06

AceEvent-3.0 loads in the libs block before core/ and is not part of LibKa0s, so it exists on a degraded load. The OnEvent body becomes `local function onBootEvent(event) ... end` with the same three branches; the PLAYER_LOGIN branch calls boot:UnregisterEvent('PLAYER_LOGIN'). Registration moves out of file load into `State.Arm()`, called from NS:OnInitialize (core/KickCD.lua, after Database:Init; ADDON_LOADED always precedes PLAYER_LOGIN) through NS.RegisterEventList(boot, { {'PLAYER_LOGIN', onBootEvent}, {'PLAYER_REGEN_DISABLED', onBootEvent}, {'PLAYER_REGEN_ENABLED', onBootEvent} }), because NS.RegisterEventList (core/CoreSetup.lua) loads after State.lua. State.StandDown is boot:UnregisterAllEvents(). State.StandUp re-registers only the REGEN pair plus the InCombatLockdown seed: delete :206-213's `if not State.__seeded then boot:RegisterEvent('PLAYER_LOGIN') end` and State.__seeded. Rewrite the comments at :131-141, :176-193 and :199-205. docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:99 loses its private-frame argument: the addon now has no private frame for ordinary events, and events-frames-taint-§1 carries no register row. tests/wow_mock.lua:520-524 and :762-767 comments no longer cite the State frame.

#### KC-18 — Adopt LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 (minor 2) as the settings seam, with writeThrough for the composed master rows ⚠

- **Effort** L · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-03, KC-16, KC-17 · **Issues** KickCD#22
- **Findings** —
- **Change** KickCD#22 plus the LK-22 and LK-23 consumer follow-ups, following the version-2 adoption note. (1) New settings/SchemaSetup.lua. TOC: first line of the settings block, before settings\Slash.lua, with '# LOAD-BEARING POSITION: creates NS.Settings.Schema (the rows array) and NS.Settings.Store, which both descriptors and every page file's add/AddComposed reach at load'. `NS.Settings.WRITE_THROUGH = { 'enabled', 'locked' }` is defined once. `NS.Settings.Store = SchemaLib:New(d)`, where SchemaLib = LibStub('LibKa0s-Schema-1.0', true) or HostSchemaStub. The descriptor d: rows = NS.Settings.Schema; resolveRoot = function() return NS.db and NS.db.profile, 1 end; writeThrough = NS.Settings.WRITE_THROUGH. announce(row, path, value): return for row.sessionOnly or row.get rows (as today, session and global writes send nothing); otherwise `local section = row.section or (row.writeThrough and 'general')`; if row.writeThrough and path == 'enabled', call NS.RefreshEnabledHold(); then Helpers.FireConfigChanged(section). announceBatch(writes) fires each distinct section once in first-seen order, replacing Helpers.Coalesced. debug is the existing per-path trailing debounce (logSet, flushPendingSets and fmtSetValue move here verbatim) for the single-write 'Set' line; any other line flushes pending lines first and then goes to NS.Debug. debugEnabled = function() return NS.State and NS.State.debug end; format = fmtSetValue; print = NS.Util.print; resetExempt = { ['global.minimap.shown'] = true }, which replaces survivesEveryReset. (2) Row wiring, all in settings/General.lua beside the visibility override (:98). The composed 'enabled' row gets `onChange = function() NS.RefreshEnabledHold() end`, which replaces Panel.lua:417. 'state.debugConsole' gets SESSION_PATHS' get/set closures. 'global.minimap.shown' gets GLOBAL_PATHS' get/set closures. units.focus.link (:152-160) gets a `set` closure that records whether the stored link moved before storing, and its onChange(value) calls H.RefreshAllPanels() only when it moved (Schema's onChange has no old value). settings/Castbar.lua:113-116's onChange writes through Store.Set('units.'..unit..'.castbar.growDirection', newGrow). (3) Seam bindings. Options descriptor (settings/OptionsSetup.lua): get = Store.Get (NS.Settings.ReadForPanel stays bound to it); set = function(p, v) local ok, e, w = Store.Set(p, v); if ok then H.RefreshScalars() end; return ok, e, w end; applyDefault through Store.ApplyDefault plus RefreshScalars; bulkBegin = flushPendingSets then Store.BulkBegin; bulkEnd = Store.BulkEnd; resetProfile = Store.ResetCounted(function() NS.db:ResetProfile() end, function(row) return row.panel ~= 'profiles' end); validate = Store.Validate({ defaultsRoot = function() return NS.DEFAULT_PROFILE, 1 end }). Slash descriptor: get = Store.Get; set = Store.Set, whose `false, err, why` Slash minor 15 prints; findRow = Store.FindRow; applyDefault = Store.ApplyDefault; bulk pair = Store's. Helpers.SetAndRefresh(path, v) stays as a two-line host helper (Store.Set, then RefreshScalars on ok, answering ok). core/Units.lua CopyStyling (:116-155): H.SetRows becomes Store.SetMany(entries, { act = 'copy', scope = from..'→'..to }), which is all-or-nothing; H.Get and H.FindSchema become Store.Get and Store.FindRow. core/Database.lua consumeResetCount calls Store.ConsumeResetCount. settings/Slash.lua GateHint's fallback probe uses SchemaLib.Read/Write on NS.db.profile. (4) Deleted. From settings/Panel.lua: Resolve, SESSION_PATHS, GLOBAL_PATHS, Helpers.Get, pending/NIL_SECTION/Coalesced, sameValue, bulkEnter/bulkLeave/logAct/MuteSetLog/BulkBegin/BulkEnd, pendingResetCount/ConsumeResetCount/countChangedProfileRows/ResetProfileCounted, Helpers.Set, Helpers.FindSchema (callers move to Store.FindRow), ValidateSchema with _validPanels/_validSections/_validTypes/_printSchemaError. From settings/Panel_Render.lua: writeRow and Helpers.SetRows. Helpers.AddComposed stamps and then calls Store.AddRows, and each page's add{} calls Store.AddRows({row}). Helpers.FireConfigChanged and SchemaForPanel stay. (5) HostSchemaStub, in SchemaSetup.lua, follows the version-2 doc's 'The degradation stub': write-completing and log-silent. Lib level: SplitPath, Read, Write, SameValue and New(d). Instance: AllRows, AddRows, FindRow, Reindex, Get, Set, SetMany, Default, ApplyDefault, BulkBegin, BulkEnd, BulkRun, BulkAdd, InBulk, CountOffDefault, ResetCounted, ConsumeResetCount and Validate. Set honors d.writeThrough, storing a copy at the path and announcing with a synthetic { path = p, writeThrough = true } built once per path, and refuses every other row-less path in the host's own words (L['Setting not found: %s']). No STRINGS copy. (6) Behavior pins from the adoption notes: an unknown path is refused, a table value is copied in, a raising onChange propagates (it was pcall-printed), and group is required on every row. The panel and section enum check moves from runtime into tests/test_schema.lua. (7) Docs: docs/settings-panel.md (write seam), docs/schema.md, docs/ARCHITECTURE.md (Settings schema, External dependencies: one more adopted major), docs/module-map.md (SchemaSetup row), docs/slash-dispatch.md. The commit body says 'Closes #22' (the owner closes it on merge).
- **Test first** Red first, in a new tests/test_schema_store.lua added to SUITES. (a) 'the settings seam is a LibKa0s-Schema-1.0 instance' (NS.Settings.Store.Set exists, Helpers.Set == nil); red. (b) 'a write announces CONFIG_CHANGED once with the row's section' (spy NS.SendMessage). (c) '`/kcd set enabled false` stands the addon down in the same turn'. (d) 'Copy styling is one SetMany: one [Set] copy target→focus: N rows line, each section announced once'. (e) 'a bad value in a batch writes nothing'. (f) 'an unknown path is refused, never stored'. (g) 'a stored table is a copy'. (h) 'a raising onChange propagates after the store'. (i) degraded, libFiles = {}: 'Store.Set("enabled", false) writes through and takes the disabled hold, with no Lua error'; 'Store.Set on any other composed path is refused'. tests/test_surface_parity.lua adds T.assertSurfaceParity(liveStore, stubStore, 'schema instance vs host stub') and T.assertSurfaceParity(HostSchemaStub, 'LibKa0s-Schema-1.0', { 'STRINGS' }), and tests/run.lua's Kit.setSurfaceSource map gains ['LibKa0s-Schema-1.0']. tests/test_schema.lua: 'every row's panel and section are known', 'Validate reports 0 errors on the shipped schema', 'every stored row resolves against DEFAULT_PROFILE'. Migrate the existing cases that call Helpers.Get/Set/FindSchema/SetRows/MuteSetLog/ResetProfileCounted/Coalesced to Store.*.

#### KC-19 — Degraded Slash stub: DisabledLine pinned to the library constant, the library-absent line, and enable/disable/lock that write through ⚠

- **Effort** M · **Depends on** RV-KC, KC-18
- **Findings** —
- **Change** LK-18 and WS-02 consumer follow-ups. settings/Slash.lua stub (:200-285). (1) `local DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT = '%s is disabled \226\128\148 enable it with \|cFFFFFF00%s\|r'` holds the verbatim bytes of LibKa0s-Slash-1.0's DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT (the Slash version-15 doc's 'The degradation stub'), exposed as stub.__disabledLineFormat for the pin; the `__` prefix keeps it outside parity. stub.DisabledLine = function() return DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT:format(d.brandName or d.slash, d.slash .. ' enable') end. stub.OnSlash refuses a verb outside d.liveVerbs with DisabledLine while d.isEnabled() is false, keeping dispatch minimal. Rewrite the :230-239 'answers nil' comment. (2) The library-absent line: locales/enUS.lua gains L['%s is unavailable: the LibKa0s library did not load.'] (WS-02, one sentence and one placeholder). absent(verb) prints it formatted with '/kcd ' .. verb, replacing the ' is unavailable. ' .. NS.LIBKA0S_MISSING concatenation. (3) stub.CliSet(rest): split into path and value. When the path is in NS.Settings.WRITE_THROUGH and the value is a bool literal (true/false/on/off; a literal check, not a copy of the library parser), it calls NS.Settings.Store.Set(path, bool) and prints `path .. ' = ' .. tostring(bool)` on success. Every other path, and any failure, prints the library-absent line for '/kcd set' and writes nothing, never raising. The enable and disable verbs (core/KickCD.lua:219-222, setSetting) are unchanged and so take route (a). (4) core/KickCD.lua setLocked (:156-161) writes through `NS.Settings.Store and NS.Settings.Store.Set('locked', v)` and then calls H.RefreshScalars if present. lock, unlock and toggle therefore work on a library-absent load; 'Settings layer not ready yet' stays for a nil Store. Rewrite the comment at :138-155. docs/slash-dispatch.md gets a degraded-verbs section; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md's Slash paragraph is updated.
- **Test first** Red first, in tests/test_slash.lua and tests/test_disabled.lua, on the degraded arm (T.load(true, true, nil, { libFiles = {} })). (a) 'the stub's DisabledLine format is the library constant, byte for byte': T.assertLibraryConstant(NS.Slash.cli.__disabledLineFormat, 'LibKa0s-Slash-1.0', 'DISABLED_LINE_FORMAT'), using the live source registered in tests/run.lua. It is red on HEAD (nil). (b) 'degraded `/kcd disable` writes enabled = false, stands down, prints the confirmation and raises nothing'; red on HEAD ('set is unavailable', enabled unchanged). (c) 'degraded `/kcd enable` brings it back up'. (d) 'degraded `/kcd lock` writes locked'; red on HEAD ('Settings layer not ready yet'). (e) 'degraded `/kcd list` prints the library-absent line' (wording pin; red on HEAD). (f) 'degraded `/kcd set visibility always` writes nothing and prints the library-absent line'. (g) 'degraded `/kcd lock` while disabled prints the DisabledLine'. Update tests/test_options_panel.lua's 'the hollow composers cost the degraded path no CLI reach' case to the new reach.
